<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/tech-policy/2026/06/sp-500-blocks-fast-spacex-entry-wont-waive-rule-for-unprofitable-ai-firms/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>S&amp;P 500 rejects SpaceX, also blocking entry for OpenAI and Anthropic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>标准普尔500拒绝 SpaceX 入选，也不放行 OpenAI 与 Anthropic</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48421442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1421</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48421442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 487</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>

It highlights how profitability, scale, and market categorization influence access to major indices, shaping AI firms’ market visibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章解释了标普500 的入选标准与流程，为何 SpaceX 未被纳入、OpenAI 与 Anthropic 面临相似的限制。指出盈利能力、规模与市场分类如何影响进入主要指数，从而影响 AI 公司的市场知名度。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48385906' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48385906' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/introducing-gemma-4-12b/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemma 4 12B: A unified, encoder-free multimodal model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemma 4 12B：一个统一的无需编码器的多模态模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 09:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48385906' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1041</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48385906' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 385</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48385906'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 12B is Google&#x27;s encoder-free, unified multimodal model designed to handle text, image, and other modalities with a single architecture. By removing dedicated encoders, it aims to simplify training and inference while enabling more flexible cross-modal tasks. This approach could lower engineering friction for developers building multimodal applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 12B 是谷歌推出的无编码器、统一的多模态模型，能够用同一架构处理文本、图像等模态。取消独立编码器的设计旨在简化训练和推理，为跨模态任务带来更大的灵活性。此类设计若成熟，可能降低多模态应用的工程门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Social_Image_G4_12B.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Social_Image_G4_12B.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48405718' data-tags='Business,Finance,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48405718' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-06-04/s-p-dow-jones-keeps-megacap-ipo-rules-as-is-after-consultation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SpaceX, Other Mega IPOs Denied Fast Index Entry by S&amp;P</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SpaceX 与其他 Mega IPO 被 S&amp;P 拒绝快速指数入口</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 15:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48405718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1017</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48405718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 503</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48405718'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>S&amp;P Dow Jones kept the existing fast-entry rules for megacap IPOs, meaning SpaceX and other large offerings won&#x27;t automatically get quicker index inclusion. The decision shapes index visibility and trading dynamics for blockbuster IPOs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>标准普尔继续维持大型 IPO 的快速进入规则，SpaceX 等巨型上市不会自动获得更快的指数纳入。此决定影响指数追踪与巨型 IPO 的市场可见度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48388324' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48388324' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://elixir-lang.org/blog/2026/06/03/elixir-v1-20-0-released/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Elixir v1.20: Now a gradually typed language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Elixir v1.20：逐步类型化语言</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 12:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48388324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 979</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48388324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 395</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48388324'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Elixir 1.20 introduces gradual typing, letting developers opt into type checks without breaking dynamic code. This enables stronger tooling and safer APIs while preserving Elixir’s flexible, productive vibe.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Elixir 1.20 引入渐进式类型系统，开发者可以在不强制改动现有动态代码的前提下逐步打上类型标记。这提升工具链的准确性，帮助接口设计更安全，同时保留 Elixir 的灵活与高效开发体验。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spamming people who are actively seeking employment is harmful and counterproductive. The piece argues for respectful, consent-based outreach by recruiters and offers practical guidelines to avoid wasting candidates’ time.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>向正在找工作的人进行垃圾式联系不仅令人厌恶，而且浪费求职者时间。文章呼吁招聘方采用有同意的、定向的沟通，并给出避免干扰的实用建议。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型正在侵蚀我的软件工程职业生涯，我不知道该怎么办</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 05:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48434312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 898</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48434312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 876</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48434312'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A software engineer voices concern that LLMs are eroding the profession, prompting a re-think of skill sets. Practical responses include leaning into AI-assisted design and architecture, or shifting toward areas where human judgment remains essential.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位软件工程师表达担忧：大型语言模型正在侵蚀这个职业，促使重新考量个人的技能组合。可行的应对路径包括更深入利用 AI 辅助的设计/架构工作，或转向需要人类判断的领域。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型如何工作</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 13:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48389360' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 883</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48389360' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48389360'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A clear primer on how LLMs function, covering tokenization, transformer architectures, training objectives, and scaling laws. It also notes common caveats like the gap between statistical patterns and grounded reasoning that matter for evaluating real-world AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对大型语言模型如何工作提供清晰入门，涵盖分词、Transformer 架构、训练目标与规模效应，并点出现实世界中需警惕的误区，如统计模式与实际推理之间的差距。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 00:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48409191' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 840</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48409191' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 535</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48409191'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post outlines a shift in how the Ladybird project is developed, focusing on new workflows, tooling, and contributor practices to accelerate iteration while maintaining quality. It explains the rationale behind changing development dynamics and what readers might expect in future releases. The change aims to improve collaboration, transparency, and build reliability for users and contributors alike.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文阐述对 Ladybird 项目开发方式的变革，聚焦新的工作流、工具链和贡献者实践，旨在在保持质量的同时加快迭代速度。文中解释了改变开发动力学的理由，以及未来版本释放中可能出现的变化。此举旨在提升协作、透明度和对用户及贡献者的可靠性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48392004' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48392004' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dailycal.org/news/campus/academics/failing-grades-soar-as-professors-see-greater-ai-usage-dwindling-math-skills-in-uc-berkeley/article_16fad0bf-02cb-4b8c-8d88-888ffd9f8608.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Failing grades soar with AI usage, dwindling math skills in Berkeley CS classes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 使用激增导致伯克利计算机系课程不及格率攀升，数学能力下降</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 17:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48392004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 791</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48392004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 753</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48392004'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Berkeley CS instructors report a spike in failing grades as AI usage increases in coursework, accompanied by a perceived decline in math skills among students. The trend underscores a broader tension between AI-assisted learning and mastery of fundamentals, suggesting the need for new assessment methods and curriculum adjustments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着 AI 在课程中的使用增加，伯克利计算机科学课程的不及格率攀升，数学基础能力也在下降。这一现象凸显了 AI 辅助学习与基础知识掌握之间的张力，促使课程设计和评估方式需要改革。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bloximages.chicago2.vip.townnews.com/dailycal.org/content/tncms/assets/v3/editorial/c/40/c40f11cf-dfb0-4973-a534-c64044165f16/6a206ddeec122.image.jpg?crop=1793%2C941%2C0%2C106&amp;resize=1200%2C630&amp;order=crop%2Cresize' data-full='https://bloximages.chicago2.vip.townnews.com/dailycal.org/content/tncms/assets/v3/editorial/c/40/c40f11cf-dfb0-4973-a534-c64044165f16/6a206ddeec122.image.jpg?crop=1793%2C941%2C0%2C106&amp;resize=1200%2C630&amp;order=crop%2Cresize' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48387270' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48387270' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theatlantic.com/philosophy/2026/06/no-artificial-intelligence-is-not-conscious/687378/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Artificial intelligence is not conscious – Ted Chiang</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人工智能并非具备意识——Ted Chiang</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 10:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48387270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 765</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48387270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1329</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48387270'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ted Chiang argues that current AI lacks true consciousness and subjective experience; he distinguishes between sophisticated pattern recognition and genuine sentience. The piece further explores ethical and policy implications, urging careful framing of AI capabilities and responsibilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ted Chiang 认为当前的人工智能并非真正具备意识或主观体验；它们只是高度复杂的模式识别系统。文章还讨论伦理与政策含义，呼吁对 AI 能力与责任进行谨慎界定。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.theatlantic.com/thumbor/fdONLsUPMiSaP_UQenVaAXAt2nI=/40x45:1998x1065/1200x625/media/img/mt/2026/06/2026_06_01_chiang_Consciousness_Trap_final_horizontal/original.gif' data-full='https://cdn.theatlantic.com/thumbor/fdONLsUPMiSaP_UQenVaAXAt2nI=/40x45:1998x1065/1200x625/media/img/mt/2026/06/2026_06_01_chiang_Consciousness_Trap_final_horizontal/original.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/RedHatInsights/javascript-clients/issues/492' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Malicious npm packages detected across Red Hat Cloud Services</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Red Hat 云服务中检测到恶意 npm 包</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 06:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48356625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 759</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48356625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 442</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers detected malicious npm packages circulating inside Red Hat Cloud Services, underscoring ongoing supply‑chain risks in the npm ecosystem. The episode reinforces the importance of dependency hygiene, reproducible builds, and source provenance checks for cloud environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全团队在 Red Hat Cloud Services 内发现恶意 npm 包，暴露了 npm 生态中持续的供应链风险。该事件强调对依赖项的严格管控、可重复构建和来源可信性的重要性，尤其在云环境中。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c93x0k194yno' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta workers can opt out of being tracked at work up to 30 min</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 员工可在工作时最多不被追踪30分钟</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 05:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48383220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 748</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48383220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 724</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48383220'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta has updated its employee monitoring policy to allow workers to opt out of being tracked for up to 30 minutes at a time. The change underscores the tension between productivity analytics and employee privacy in big tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 更新员工监控政策，允许员工单次最多有30分钟不被追踪。此举凸显了大科技公司在生产力分析与员工隐私之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/947f/live/8311e710-5f35-11f1-b9e6-7bbc2b9cb9c1.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/947f/live/8311e710-5f35-11f1-b9e6-7bbc2b9cb9c1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://burntsushi.net/encephalitis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I was recently diagnosed with anti-NMDA receptor encephalitis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我最近被诊断患上抗 NMDA 受体脑炎</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 07:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48384355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 740</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48384355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 248</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48384355'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal account of being diagnosed with anti-NMDA receptor encephalitis, a rare autoimmune condition that can mimic psychiatric illness. The post emphasizes the difficulty of correct diagnosis and the importance of awareness among clinicians and patients. It sheds light on the intersection of neurology and mental health.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者分享罕见的抗 NMDA 受体脑炎诊断经历，这种疾病常因症状类似精神疾病而误诊。文章强调正确诊断的难度，以及提高临床与患者对该病的认知重要性，彰显神经科与精神健康的交叉议题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48353348'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A decade-old Xeon can still power modern workloads if software is tuned for efficiency. The post demonstrates competitive results on a 2016-era server CPU, encouraging a rethink of hardware budgets for modest-scale deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>经过优化的软件仍能让十年前的 Xeon 功能不错地应对现代工作负载。该案例在 2016 年代的服务器 CPU 上展现出具有竞争力的性能，促使小型部署重新考量硬件预算。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48364055' data-tags='Finance,Business,AI,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48364055' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/finance-and-economics/2026/06/01/can-the-stockmarket-swallow-anthropic-spacex-and-openai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can the stockmarket swallow Anthropic, SpaceX and OpenAI?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>股市能否容纳Anthropic、SpaceX与OpenAI？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 16:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48364055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 699</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48364055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1233</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48364055'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Economist asks whether public markets have the capacity to absorb the outsized valuations and funding needs of Anthropic, SpaceX, and OpenAI. It highlights structural constraints like liquidity, dilution, and investor risk appetite that could slow up-rounds for highly valued private firms. The takeaway is that public markets may cap how quickly such companies can scale via public offerings, shaping their financing strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文章质疑公开市场是否具备吸收Anthropic、SpaceX与OpenAI等估值膨胀企业及其融资需求的能力。它强调流动性、股本稀释和投资者风险偏好等结构性约束，可能放缓高估值私有公司的轮融资。核心观点是公开市场可能限制这些公司通过上市快速扩张的能力，从而影响它们的融资策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48345840' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Web,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48345840' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hacktivis.me/articles/cloudflare-turnstile-webgl-fingerprinting' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare Turnstile requiring fingerprintable WebGL</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare Turnstile 要求可指纹化的 WebGL</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 07:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48345840' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 694</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48345840' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 395</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48345840'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare Turnstile now reportedly relies on fingerprintable WebGL, enabling GPU-based device fingerprinting. That blurs the line between anti-bot verification and user surveillance, raising privacy concerns for developers and users who expected a privacy-friendly CAPTCHA alternative. Expect calls for opt-outs or mitigations in browsers and privacy tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称 Cloudflare Turnstile 现正使用可指纹化的 WebGL，借助 GPU 渲染实施设备指纹。这模糊了反机器人验证与用户监控之间的界线，引发对隐私的担忧，尤其是对期待更隐私友好验证码的用户与开发者。浏览器和隐私工具可能会提出规避或缓解措施的呼声。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hacktivis.me/images/avatar.png?serial=2020111201' data-full='https://hacktivis.me/images/avatar.png?serial=2020111201' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 16:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48406174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 694</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48406174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1077</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48398055' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/voidzero-joins-cloudflare/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VoidZero Is Joining Cloudflare</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>VoidZero 加入 Cloudflare</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 06:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48398055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 677</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48398055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48398055'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare welcomes VoidZero to its ecosystem, expanding its network of partners and collaborators. The arrangement is expected to bring VoidZero&#x27;s capabilities into Cloudflare&#x27;s edge and security stack, enabling joint tooling for developers building on Cloudflare&#x27;s platform. This underscores Cloudflare&#x27;s strategy of growing a robust, partner-driven developer ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 宣布 VoidZero 加入其生态系统，作为扩展开发者生态和安全协作的一部分。双方的整合将把 VoidZero 的技术引入 Cloudflare 的边缘与安全栈，帮助开发者在 Cloudflare 平台上构建与保护应用。此举体现 Cloudflare 持续打造以伙伴为驱动的生态策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/77PFBBDzFzIVtsPi4GXm5g/ea71873167012a47518dc593033032c6/BLOG-VOID_OG.png' data-full='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/77PFBBDzFzIVtsPi4GXm5g/ea71873167012a47518dc593033032c6/BLOG-VOID_OG.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48382310' data-tags='Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48382310' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.nns.ee/2026/06/03/katana-badusb/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pwnd Blaster: Hacking your PC using your speaker without ever touching it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hacking your PC using your speaker without ever touching it</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 03:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48382310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 677</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48382310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48382310'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate a BadUSB-style attack that uses a speaker to compromise a PC without any direct user interaction. The technique underlines how trusted hardware can be weaponized through subtle channels, raising concerns about supply chain, peripherals, and device authentication. It highlights the risk of peripheral-based exploits and the need for hardware-level defenses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员展示了利用扬声器进行 BadUSB 式攻击、在无需任何直接交互的情况下入侵电脑的可能性。这一技巧暴露了受信任硬件可被通过微妙通道 weaponize 的风险，凸显了外设信任、供应链与硬件认证方面的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.nns.ee/img/2026-04-08-katana-badusb/og-image.png' data-full='https://blog.nns.ee/img/2026-04-08-katana-badusb/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48427643' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48427643' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://this.weekinsecurity.com/meta-confirms-thousands-of-instagram-accounts-were-hacked-by-abusing-its-ai-chatbot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta confirms 1000s of Instagram accounts were hacked by abusing its AI chatbot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 确认数千 Instagram 账号因滥用其 AI 聊天机器人而被入侵</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 11:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48427643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 674</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48427643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48427643'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Thousands of Instagram accounts were compromised as attackers abused an AI chatbot feature, exposing new attack vectors for prompt injection and social engineering. The incident underscores security risks in consumer AI assistants and the need for safer safeguards and monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 确认数千个 Instagram 账号因滥用其 AI 聊天机器人而被入侵，暴露出通过提示注入与社交工程的攻击向量。事件凸显消费级 AI 助手在安全性方面的风险，以及对更安全防护与监控的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ed/a2/eda2c6f7-faef-48b4-9ed4-86a4fa4dca68/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/meta-ai-chatbot-twis-story-2.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ed/a2/eda2c6f7-faef-48b4-9ed4-86a4fa4dca68/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/meta-ai-chatbot-twis-story-2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48368121' data-tags='Legal,Open Source,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48368121' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.adafruit.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Adafruit receives demand letter from Fenwick legal counsel on behalf of Flux.ai</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Adafruit 收到 Flux.ai 代表的 Fenwick 律师事务所律师函</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 03:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48368121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 665</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48368121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 273</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48368121'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Adafruit reports receiving a formal demand letter from Fenwick &amp; West on behalf of Flux.ai, signaling a potential IP or licensing dispute. The letter underscores how IP questions can affect hobbyist hardware vendors and the broader open-source hardware ecosystem. Readers should watch for updates on how Adafruit responds and what this means for developers and retailers shipping AI-enabled hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Adafruit 表示收到了 Flux.ai 代表的 Fenwick 律师事务所发出的正式函件，暗示可能存在知识产权或许可方面的争议。此类函件凸显了知识产权问题对爱好者硬件厂商及开源硬件生态的潜在影响。请关注 Adafruit 的回应及此事对开发者和销售 AI 硬件的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn-blog.adafruit.com/uploads/2026/06/ptlimor.jpeg' data-full='https://cdn-blog.adafruit.com/uploads/2026/06/ptlimor.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48371562' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48371562' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.ammaraskar.com/github-token-stealing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1-Click GitHub Token Stealing via a VSCode Bug</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过 VSCode 漏洞的一键式 GitHub 令牌窃取</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 08:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48371562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 648</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48371562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48371562'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A VSCode bug exposes GitHub tokens with a single click, enabling token theft via the IDE’s authentication flow. The incident underscores the risk of embedded auth in development tools and token-scoping hygiene; users should rotate tokens, enable 2FA, and update to patched versions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>VSCode 中的一个漏洞使攻击者能够在一次点击中窃取 GitHub 访问令牌，利用集成的认证流程进行攻击。这一事件暴露了开发工具中嵌入式认证的风险以及令牌作用域带来的安全隐患。用户应轮换令牌、启用两步验证并更新到修补版本，同时谨慎管理令牌权限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/9fdd32c8051eaa1dc4f2cc06186cb85d?s=200' data-full='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/9fdd32c8051eaa1dc4f2cc06186cb85d?s=200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48348578' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48348578' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/i/status/2060746160558543217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex just found a &quot;workaround&quot; of not having sudo on my PC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Codex 找到一个无需 sudo 就能在我的电脑工作的方法</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 11:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48348578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 647</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48348578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 307</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48348578'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reveal shows Codex-powered coding workflows finding ways to perform privileged tasks without sudo, hinting at potential privilege-escalation risks. It highlights why secure defaults and careful permission handling matter when AI-generated code is used.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>透露显示通过 Codex 的工作流，可在不使用 sudo 的情况下完成特权任务，提示潜在的特权提升风险。强调在使用 AI 代码时，需依赖安全默认设置和严格权限控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HJk9teSXMAEwKuF.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HJk9teSXMAEwKuF.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48392232' data-tags='Science,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48392232' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://e360.yale.edu/digest/trump-ooi-amoc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. to dismantle system tracking Atlantic currents that are at risk of collapse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国将拆除对濒临崩溃风险的大西洋环流监测系统</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 17:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48392232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 622</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48392232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 472</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48392232'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Policy moves to retire the monitoring system tracking Atlantic currents amid concerns about AMOC stability, raising questions about data availability for climate forecasting and research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于美国计划拆除对大西洋环流监测的系统，担忧 AMOC 可能崩溃，数据缺失将影响气候预测与研究。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48357154' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48357154' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://torrentfreak.com/the-pirate-bay-remains-resilient-20-years-after-the-raid/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Pirate Bay Remains Resilient, 20 Years After the Raid</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>海盗湾在遭突袭二十年后仍然顽强</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 07:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48357154' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 607</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48357154' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 304</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48357154'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Pirate Bay remains active two decades after a major raid, evolving with mirrors and resilient access methods. The story highlights ongoing demand for piracy and the challenges of shutting down persistent platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>海盗湾在被突袭二十年后依然活跃，通过镜像站和多样化的访问方式继续运作。这反映出对盗版的持续需求以及关闭持续存在的平台的难度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://torrentfreak.com/images/tpbraidfeat.png' data-full='https://torrentfreak.com/images/tpbraidfeat.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48383056' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48383056' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Jun/3/uber-caps-usage/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Uber&#x27;s $1,500/month AI limit is a useful signal for AI tool pricing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Uber 将 AI 工具使用额度设定为每月 1500 美元，为定价提供信号</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 05:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48383056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 597</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48383056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 728</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48383056'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Uber caps AI tool usage at $1,500 per month, a concrete pricing signal for enterprise AI tooling. The cap affects individual power users and teams alike, influencing how startups and enterprises budget for copilots, assistants, and automation. It underscores the market’s sensitivity to costs and value at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Uber 将 AI 工具使用额度设定为每月 1500 美元，为企业级 AI 工具定价提供了明确信号。该上限影响个人与团队的使用成本，促使初创公司与企业在成本与价值之间权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48415217' data-tags='Business,Finance,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48415217' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/public-sector/2026/06/04/govuk-goes-dutch-on-payments-as-it-dumps-stripe/5250763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gov.uk has replaced Stripe with Dutch provider Adyen</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GOV.UK 已用 Adyen 取代 Stripe 的支付服务</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 09:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48415217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 571</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48415217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48415217'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GOV.UK replaced Stripe with Adyen for payments, indicating a public-sector shift to different payment providers. The change may affect developers integrating with government payments and reflects considerations like cost, reliability, and multi-region coverage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GOV.UK 将支付服务从 Stripe 切换至 Adyen，显示公共部门在支付供应商选择上的调整。对开发者集成政府支付的方式可能有影响，并反映成本、可靠性和区域覆盖等因素的考量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/252660.jpg?imageId=252660&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/252660.jpg?imageId=252660&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48383667' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48383667' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mouseless.click' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mouseless – keyboard-driven control of macOS/Linux/Windows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mouseless—通过键盘控制 macOS/Linux/Windows</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 06:22 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>From addiction, prison, and a felony, the author documents rebuilding life and career from scratch. Through skill-building, persistence, and supportive networks, the story offers practical insights for reintegration and second chances in tech.</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48427523' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 546</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48427523' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 432</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Pentagon reportedly elevated the risk level of Israeli spying on the U.S. to the highest tier, signaling heightened concern within defense and intelligence communities. The move could affect security cooperation and influence internal policies on intelligence-sharing and counter-espionage. It underscores how geopolitical tensions ripple into tech and data security considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称，五角大楼将以色列对美情报活动的威胁等级提高到最高级别，反映出防务与情报机构的担忧升级。此举可能影响两国在情报共享与安全合作方面的政策走向，并推动相关防范与反情报措施的调整。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48358646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 529</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48358646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 445</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic filed a confidential draft S-1 with the SEC, signaling intent to pursue an IPO. The move comes as AI safety-focused startups navigate a crowded funding environment and evolving regulatory expectations.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48346947' data-tags='Health,Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48346947' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thesciverse.org/scientists-found-that-the-creatine-supplement-millions-take-for-muscle-gains-is-quietly-raising-brain-energy-levels-and-slowing-early-alzheimers-cognitive-decline-by-30/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Creatine raises brain energy levels and slows cognitive decline: study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>肌酸提升大脑能量并减缓认知衰退的研究</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 09:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48346947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 526</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48346947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 367</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48346947'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds creatine supplementation boosts brain energy and slows cognitive decline, potentially by up to 30% in early Alzheimer&#x27;s progression. If confirmed, this could influence dietary guidelines and therapeutic strategies for neurodegenerative diseases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示补充肌酸可以提升大脑能量并减缓认知衰退，早期阿尔茨海默病可能下降速度最高可达约30%。若再证实，这将影响膳食建议和神经退行性疾病的治疗策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://thesciverse.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Scientists-found-that-the-creatine-supplement-millions-take-for-muscle-gains-is-quietly-raising-brain-energy-levels-and-slowing-early-Alzheimers-cognitive-decline-by-30.webp' data-full='http://thesciverse.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Scientists-found-that-the-creatine-supplement-millions-take-for-muscle-gains-is-quietly-raising-brain-energy-levels-and-slowing-early-Alzheimers-cognitive-decline-by-30.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48403980' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48403980' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/defending-code-reference-harness' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic&#x27;s open-source framework for AI-powered vulnerability discovery</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 开源框架用于 AI 驱动的漏洞发现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 13:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48403980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 524</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48403980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48403980'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic released an open-source framework to aid AI-assisted vulnerability discovery in software. The toolkit aims to standardize how researchers build, run, and compare AI-driven security analyses, promoting reproducible, faster security research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 发布了一个开源框架，用以辅助 AI 驱动的软件漏洞发现。该工具集旨在标准化研究者如何构建、运行并比较 AI 相关的安全分析，推动可重复的漏洞研究并可能加快修复进程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6e3145d29b1b19416b94866819bde9eaa223e4d09e14595ba432f1c3abba8d6e/anthropics/defending-code-reference-harness' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6e3145d29b1b19416b94866819bde9eaa223e4d09e14595ba432f1c3abba8d6e/anthropics/defending-code-reference-harness' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/institute/recursive-self-improvement' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When AI Builds Itself: Our progress toward recursive self-improvement</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 自我构建：向递归自我提升推进的进展</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 09:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48400842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 514</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48400842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 688</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48400842'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic outlines progress in recursive self-improvement, a scenario where AI systems iteratively enhance themselves with reduced human input. The discussion touches on safety, alignment, and governance challenges as capabilities scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 概述递归自我提升的进展，即 AI 系统在尽量减少人类介入的情况下迭代自我改进。讨论涉及能力提升带来的安全、对齐与治理挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/65697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic, please ship an official Claude Desktop for Linux</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48434436' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 484</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48434436' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 276</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48434436'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>There is a call for an official Linux desktop app for Claude; currently users rely on web or unofficial options, creating cross-platform friction. A native client would improve performance, privacy, and workflow integration for developers and researchers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>呼吁为 Claude 发布官方 Linux 桌面应用；目前多数用户依赖网页或非官方方案，跨平台工作流受阻。原生客户端将提升性能、隐私和开发者工作流整合。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48375824' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48375824' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lumafield.com/scan-of-the-month/byd' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CT scans of BYD car parts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>比亚迪汽车部件的 CT 扫描</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 13:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48375824' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 482</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48375824' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 372</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48375824'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>High-resolution CT scans reveal internal features and potential manufacturing artifacts in BYD car parts, showcasing the power of non-destructive imaging for QA and design. Such scans help engineers verify tolerances and spot hidden defects without disassembly, enabling faster QA and reverse engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>高分辨率的 CT 扫描揭示了比亚迪汽车部件的内部结构与潜在制造缺陷，展现出无损成像在 QA 与设计优化中的作用。此类扫描帮助工程师核验公差、发现隐藏的制造痕迹，并在不拆解部件的情况下推进逆向工程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/63e15418201b6e2a5cabb911/6a1a332aa8018a5104366420_og-image-sotm-byd.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/63e15418201b6e2a5cabb911/6a1a332aa8018a5104366420_og-image-sotm-byd.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48411635' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48411635' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alexispurslane.github.io/rsync-analysis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Did Claude increase bugs in rsync?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 是否在 rsync 中引入更多漏洞？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 05:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48411635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 475</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48411635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 478</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48411635'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis questions whether using Claude correlates with more bugs in rsync, raising questions about AI-assisted tooling reliability in critical utilities. It underscores the need for rigorous testing when integrating AI copilots.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析指出在 rsync 中使用 Claude 可能带来更多漏洞，这凸显了在关键工具中使用 AI 助手时对可靠性和充分测试的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://alexispurslane.github.io/rsync-analysis/images/github-issue.png' data-full='https://alexispurslane.github.io/rsync-analysis/images/github-issue.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48414367' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48414367' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/microsoft/pg_durable' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>pg_durable: Microsoft open sources in-database durable execution</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>pg_durable：微软开源在数据库内的持久执行</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 08:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48414367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 464</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48414367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48414367'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft open-sourced pg_durable, a project aiming to provide durable, in-database execution for PostgreSQL. It tackles fault tolerance for long-running analytics and complex workflows by keeping computation results persistent within the database layer.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软开源 pg_durable，目标是在 PostgreSQL 中实现持久化的数据库内执行。该项目通过在数据库层持久化计算结果，提升长时间分析与复杂工作流的容错性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/72d46c21036def48cf49d4980cd9f5bf3d22226abd4eb3b03a7e0b4b1c50c0d4/microsoft/pg_durable' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/72d46c21036def48cf49d4980cd9f5bf3d22226abd4eb3b03a7e0b4b1c50c0d4/microsoft/pg_durable' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48377404' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48377404' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/c0dejedi/nbd-vram' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Use your Nvidia GPU&#x27;s VRAM as swap space on Linux</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Linux 上将 Nvidia GPU 的 VRAM 用作交换空间</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 15:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48377404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 463</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48377404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48377404'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Linux tool exposes Nvidia GPU VRAM as a swap device via NBD, letting systems swap to GPU memory under memory pressure. It&#x27;s a niche hack that can extend usable RAM on GPUs with large VRAM but performance depends on workload and memory bandwidth. Not a replacement for main RAM, but a clever hack for specific workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款 Linux 工具通过 NBD 将 Nvidia GPU 的 VRAM 暂时作为交换空间使用。对于显存较大的显卡来说，在内存压力时可以延迟将数据写入磁盘，但它并非主 RAM 的替代品，性能取决于 workload 与带宽。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f707041470b206e290dfdebc289a69e72df4167d7d03b1ae720b11d4d43f7cf5/c0deJedi/nbd-vram' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f707041470b206e290dfdebc289a69e72df4167d7d03b1ae720b11d4d43f7cf5/c0deJedi/nbd-vram' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48422798' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/d/36134-grapheneos-user-reported-to-authorities-for-using-grapheneos' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GrapheneOS user reported to authorities for using GrapheneOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>因使用 GrapheneOS 而被举报给当局的用户</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 01:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48422798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 443</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48422798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 470</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48422798'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Privacy-focused mobile OS choices remain sensitive in some jurisdictions, as this case shows a GrapheneOS user being reported to authorities simply for using it. The incident illustrates how tool choices for privacy can attract scrutiny, raising concerns about chilling effects and regulatory overreach. It underscores the ongoing tension between privacy, security tools, and law enforcement practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在部分地区，隐私友好型手机系统的使用仍然敏感。仅因使用 GrapheneOS 就被举报，显示出对隐私工具的监管压力和潜在的压制性后果。此事凸显了隐私保护与执法之间的紧张关系，以及对开源安全工具的合规边界需要清晰界定。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/opengraph.png' data-full='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/opengraph.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48397233' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.france24.com/en/culture/20260604-french-iranian-author-marjane-satrapi-author-of-persepolis-dies-at-56' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>French-Iranian author Marjane Satrapi, author of &#x27;Persepolis&#x27;, dies at 56</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国-伊朗作家玛尔詹娜·萨特拉皮去世，享年56岁</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 04:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48397233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 440</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48397233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48397233'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Marjane Satrapi, the acclaimed author of Persepolis, dies at 56. Her graphic memoir bridged personal history and political upheaval, influencing generations of readers and artists worldwide. Her death marks a significant loss for comics, literature, and diasporic voices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>著名图像小说《 Persepolis 》作者玛尔詹娜·萨特拉皮去世，享年56岁。她以图像化的自传叙述将个人经历与政治动荡相连，影响了全球无数读者与艺术家。她的离世对漫画、文学和侨民声音而言都是重大损失。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s.france24.com/media/display/4b6a9198-5feb-11f1-8dfd-005056a90284/w:1280/p:16x9/000-34LF8ZA.jpg' data-full='https://s.france24.com/media/display/4b6a9198-5feb-11f1-8dfd-005056a90284/w:1280/p:16x9/000-34LF8ZA.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48420827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: Why is the HN crowd so anti-AI?</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 19:31 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The discussion surfaces why hackers on HN push back on AI hype—safety concerns, data usage, and misinformation—reflecting a culture wary of unverified breakthroughs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论揭示了 Hacker News 社区对 AI 热潮的抵触原因：安全性、数据使用以及错误信息等担忧，折射出对未经证实突破的谨慎态度。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/live/c4g44ew3g1kt' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Astronauts told to return to ISS after sheltering over air leak repairs</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 08:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48413464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 417</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48413464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 263</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48413464'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astronauts were told to return to the ISS after sheltering during air leak repairs. The move underscores ongoing maintenance risks in space habitats and how teams manage crew safety during contingencies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>宇航员在对空气泄漏进行修复并避难后，被指示返回国际空间站。这一安排凸显太空栖息地的持续维护风险，以及地面团队在应对突发情况时对乘员安全的管理。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/pc-components/ddr5/32gb-of-ddr5-now-costs-usd375-minimum-ai-shortage-continues-to-squeeze-pc-building' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>32GB of DDR5 now costs $375 – AI shortage continues to squeeze PC building</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>32GB DDR5现价375美元—AI短缺持续挤压PC组装成本</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 05:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48383241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 413</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48383241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 370</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48383241'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DDR5 memory for 32GB modules has risen to around $375, reflecting AI-driven demand pressures. The price squeeze affects DIY PC builders and smaller workloads, nudging buyers toward different configurations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>32GB DDR5 内存现价约375美元，反映 AI 相关需求的上行压力。价格紧缩影响 DIY PC 构建者与小型工作负载，推动用户调整为其他配置。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/G4YHFng8EzKzoEAcjKCD8k-2560-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/G4YHFng8EzKzoEAcjKCD8k-2560-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48423990' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/05/google-will-pay-spacex-920m-per-month-for-compute/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google will pay SpaceX $920M per month for compute</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌将每月向 SpaceX 支付 9.2 亿美元用于计算资源</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 04:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48423990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 409</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48423990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48423990'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google is striking a blockbuster deal, paying SpaceX about $920 million per month for compute capacity. The arrangement signals that AI workloads are increasingly sourced from a mix of traditional and non-traditional infrastructure providers, expanding the market for space- and edge-enabled resources. It highlights how hyperscalers are diversifying capacity to secure AI throughput at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌与 SpaceX 签下了一笔每月约9.2亿美元的计算资源采购协议，显示 AI 计算正从传统基础设施扩展到更广泛的提供者。此举凸显云巨头在扩大容量来源上的多元化，甚至包括空间/边缘资源，以应对大规模 AI 工作负载的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/GettyImages-2259661359.jpg?w=1024' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/GettyImages-2259661359.jpg?w=1024' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nvidia.com/en-us/products/rtx-spark/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia RTX Spark</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NVIDIA RTX Spark</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 22:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48352939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 408</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48352939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 404</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48352939'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA unveils RTX Spark, a platform aimed at accelerating AI workloads with specialized hardware and software integration. The release could influence developer tools, deployment pipelines, and edge AI workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA 发布 RTX Spark 平台，面向加速 AI 工作负载，整合硬件与软件生态。可能影响开发工具、部署流水线和边缘 AI 的应用场景。</p>
