<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48346257' data-tags='AI,Hardware,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48346257' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prismml.com/news/bonsai-image-4b' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1-Bit Bonsai Image 4B Image Generation for Local Devices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1-Bit Bonsai：在本地设备上的 4B 图像生成</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 08:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48346257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 406</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48346257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 170</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48346257'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 4B-parameter image generation model dubbed 1-Bit Bonsai runs on consumer hardware, enabling on-device, offline image synthesis. This kind of lightweight model lowers hardware and privacy barriers for on-device AI workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个名为 1-Bit Bonsai 的 4B 参数图像生成模型能够在普通设备上离线运行，实现在本地完成图片合成。此类轻量化模型降低了对高性能显卡的依赖，提升了隐私和离线工作流的可用性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/697a3312d33c2cc715ec3899/69961847735f3ec32aafc18e_prism-logo.svg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/697a3312d33c2cc715ec3899/69961847735f3ec32aafc18e_prism-logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48377761' data-tags='AI,Education,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48377761' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://law.stanford.edu/press/ai-outperforms-law-professors-in-stanford-law-study/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI outperforms law professors in Stanford Law study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 在斯坦福法学院研究中超越法学教授</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 16:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48377761' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 406</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48377761' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 355</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48377761'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Stanford study finds AI models outperform law professors on certain tasks, highlighting AI’s growing capabilities in legal reasoning and research. The result raises questions about law-school pedagogy and underscores the need for guardrails and human judgment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>斯坦福法学院的一项研究显示，在某些法律任务中，AI 的表现可与甚至超过法学教授，凸显了法律推理与检索能力的快速提升，同时也强调治理与人类监督的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://law.stanford.edu/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/GettyImages-2224544837-1200x630.jpg' data-full='https://law.stanford.edu/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/GettyImages-2224544837-1200x630.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48409664' data-tags='Science,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48409664' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tracing a powerful GNSS interference source over Europe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>追踪欧洲的强力 GNSS 干扰源</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 01:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48409664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 406</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48409664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 212</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48409664'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers trace a powerful GNSS interference source over Europe, characterizing the signal and narrowing down its likely origins. The work has implications for aviation, autonomous navigation, and security, illustrating how signal analysis can localize and mitigate outages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员在欧洲追踪一处强烈的 GNSS 干扰源，对信号特征进行建模并初步定位来源。这项工作对航空、自动导航与安全领域影响深远，展示了通过信号分析定位和缓解中断的可能性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48408016' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48408016' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://herbsutter.com/2026/06/04/c-the-documentary-released-today/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>C++: The Documentary</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C++：纪录片</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 21:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48408016' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 405</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48408016' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 302</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48408016'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The documentary traces the evolution of C++, from its early design goals to modern standards, featuring insights from key figures and code milestones. It situates the language&#x27;s lasting appeal in its blend of performance, control, and ongoing standards work, explaining why it remains essential for systems programming. The piece also touches on debates about complexity and ecosystem maturity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这部纪录片追溯 C++ 的演变，从早期设计目标到现代标准，采访了关键人物并回顾了重要代码里程碑。它将语言的持久魅力归因于性能、控制力与持续的标准化工作，以及为何在系统编程领域仍然不可或缺。文中也触及关于复杂性与生态系统成熟度的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://img.youtube.com/vi/lI7tMxzSJ7w/0.jpg' data-full='http://img.youtube.com/vi/lI7tMxzSJ7w/0.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48369980' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://coveillance.org/a-walking-tour-of-surveillance-infrastructure-in-seattle/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A walking tour of surveillance infrastructure in Seattle (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西雅图监控基础设施的步行导览（2020）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 06:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48369980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 402</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48369980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 302</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48369980'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A walk-through of Seattle’s surveillance landscape reveals cameras, sensors, and data flows woven into city life. It highlights how pervasive infrastructure shapes behavior and raises questions about accountability in urban monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇对西雅图监控基础设施的步行导览展示了城市中遍布的摄像头、传感器与数据流。该景观凸显监控基础设施如何影响人们的行为，并引发对城市治理与问责的思考。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48399332' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48399332' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/05/20/in-a-first-wind-solar-generated-more-power-than-gas-globally-april-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wind and solar generated more power than gas globally in April 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球2026年4月风能和太阳能发电量首次超过天然气</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 07:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48399332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 400</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48399332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 384</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48399332'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wind and solar surpassed natural gas as the largest source of global power generation in April 2026, marking a milestone for the energy transition. The surge reflects ongoing growth and affordability of renewables, but gas remains a backstop for reliability and volatility in the grid.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在2026年4月，风能与太阳能合计成为全球最大的发电来源，标志着能源转型的重要里程碑。此增长反映了可再生能源的持续扩张和成本下降，但在不稳定时段，天然气仍是电网的回补能源。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2026/03/SunZia-Vestas-wind-turbines.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2026/03/SunZia-Vestas-wind-turbines.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48414653' data-tags='AI,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48414653' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/quantization-aware-training-gemma-4/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemma 4 QAT models: Optimizing compression for mobile and laptop efficiency</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemma 4 QAT 模型：为移动端与笔记本优化压缩</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 09:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48414653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 398</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48414653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48414653'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 introduces quantization-aware training (QAT) models that optimize compression for lighter inference on mobile and laptop devices. The approach aims to shrink model size and energy use while preserving accuracy, enabling better on-device AI performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 引入量化感知训练（QAT）模型，针对移动端和笔记本实现更高效的压缩，缩减模型体积与能耗，同时尽量保持精度。这将推动在边缘设备上更实用的本地 AI 推理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Hero_Visual_Blog.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Hero_Visual_Blog.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48364800' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48364800' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.hopefullyuseful.com/blog/macos-needs-its-grid-back/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>macOS needs its grid back</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>macOS 需要回归网格布局</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 18:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48364800' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 394</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48364800' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 258</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48364800'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>macOS has drifted away from grid-based window management, making layouts feel unpredictable for power users. A consistent grid or snap-to-grid approach could restore predictable workflows and improve multitasking on the desktop. Without a stable grid, layouts suffer as apps proliferate and screen sizes vary.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>macOS 的窗口布局系统已经失去原有的网格化管理，导致高效工作流变得不稳定。文章主张回归网格对齐或引入更灵活的平铺布局，以提升多任务处理的效率。随着屏幕尺寸和应用数量的增加，缺乏稳定网格的工作流将更加低效。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.hopefullyuseful.com/blog/macos-needs-its-grid-back/picker32.jpg' data-full='https://blog.hopefullyuseful.com/blog/macos-needs-its-grid-back/picker32.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48350598' data-tags='Hardware,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48350598' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tylercipriani.com/blog/2026/05/28/chuwi-minibook-x/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chuwi Minibook X</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chuwi Minibook X</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 15:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48350598' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 394</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48350598' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 296</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48350598'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chuwi&#x27;s Minibook X continues the brand&#x27;s budget-friendly ultrabook line with a compact footprint and entry-level specs. It highlights ongoing demand for portable, affordable laptops in budget-conscious markets and the trade-offs between price, battery life, and performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chuwi Minibook X 延续了品牌的经济型超极本路线，体积紧凑、配置入门。它体现了在预算友好市场对便携笔记本的持续需求，以及价格、续航和性能之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://photos.tylercipriani.com/thumbs/58/ec9d31f64e7d203a6d40603d8ec352/medium.jpg' data-full='https://photos.tylercipriani.com/thumbs/58/ec9d31f64e7d203a6d40603d8ec352/medium.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48392343' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48392343' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kasra.blog/blog/i-spent-1500-seeing-if-llms-could-hack-my-app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I built a vulnerable app and spent $1,500 seeing if LLMs could hack it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我构建了一个易被利用的应用，花了1500美元测试大型语言模型是否能入侵</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 17:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48392343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 394</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48392343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 213</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48392343'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>I built a vulnerable app and spent $1,500 seeing if LLMs could hack it. The experiment demonstrates the real security risks of AI-assisted tooling and how quickly attackers might automate vulnerability discovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>我构建了一个易被利用的应用，花了1500美元测试大型语言模型是否能入侵。实验揭示了利用 AI 辅助工具进行安全攻击的现实风险，以及攻击者自动化漏洞利用的潜在速度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kasra.blog/favicon-512.png' data-full='https://kasra.blog/favicon-512.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48428025' data-tags='Open Source,Programming,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48428025' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ntsc.rs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ntsc-rs – open-source video emulation of analog TV and VHS artifacts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NTSC-rs：开源的模拟电视与VHS伪影视频仿真</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 12:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48428025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 394</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48428025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 118</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48428025'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NTSC-rs is an open-source Rust library that emulates the visual quirks of analog NTSC and VHS signals, including color bleed, dot crawl, and tracking jitter. It provides a toolset for researchers, retro-game developers, and artists to recreate authentic vintage video aesthetics or build preservation/test pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NTSC-rs 是一个用 Rust 编写的开源库，用于仿真 NTSC 与 VHS 信号的视觉伪影，如颜色渗色、点状爬行和追踪抖动。它为研究者、复古游戏开发者和艺术家提供重现真实老式视频风格的工具链，并有助于数字化保存与测试流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ntsc.rs/assets/images/dMI5kPlcVt-400.webp' data-full='https://ntsc.rs/assets/images/dMI5kPlcVt-400.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48363765' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48363765' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/cyberpapiii/chipotlai-max' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chipotlai Max</a></p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48401965' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48401965' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rozumem.xyz/posts/16' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The desperation of NYTimes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约时报的绝望</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 10:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48401965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 388</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48401965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 317</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48401965'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that the New York Times shows signs of desperation as it navigates the digital media era. It points to aggressive monetization, sensational framing, and strategic missteps as indicators of an older newsroom scrambling to stay dominant.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为《纽约时报》在数字时代显露出越来越多的绝望迹象。文中指向商业化营收、耸人听闻的报道与错位的战略，反映出老牌新闻机构在努力维持主导地位时的困境。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48367904' data-tags='DevOps,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48367904' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.tjll.net/you-dont-love-systemd-timers-enough/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Love systemd timers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你还没足够爱上 Systemd 定时器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 02:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48367904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 382</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48367904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48367904'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that systemd timers are underappreciated compared to traditional cron or other schedulers. It outlines advantages like finer-grained control, dependency management, and easy logging, with practical examples for daily admin use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇博文认为对 systemd 定时器的认可还不够高，甚至慢于人们对 cron 的使用热情。它总结了 systemd 定时器的优点，如更细粒度的控制、依赖管理和简化日志记录，并给出工作中的实际用例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.tjll.net/assets/images/cave.png' data-full='https://blog.tjll.net/assets/images/cave.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48432199' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48432199' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ioccc.org/2025/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The 29th International Obfuscated C Code Contest (IOCCC) 2025 Winners</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>第29届国际混淆C代码竞赛（IOCCC）2025 获奖作品</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 22:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48432199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 381</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48432199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48432199'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IOCCC 2025 winners showcase the year&#x27;s boldest obfuscated C programs, pushing compilers and tricks to the extreme. Entries rely on preprocessor gymnastics, clever data encodings, and unusual memory tricks to fit a program into tiny form factors. They illustrate C&#x27;s expressive boundaries while underscoring the importance of readability in real-world code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2025 年 IOCCC 获奖作品展现了年度最具创造性的混淆 C 代码，常用预处理器花招、另类数据表示和内存布局技巧。此类作品凸显 C 的灵活性，同时也提醒在实际软件开发中注重可读性与可维护性的重要性，并展示数十年演进后的仍具实现空间的编程模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.ioccc.org/png/hamburger-icon-open.png' data-full='https://www.ioccc.org/png/hamburger-icon-open.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://performance.dev/how-is-linear-so-fast-a-technical-breakdown' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How&#x27;s Linear so fast? A technical breakdown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linear 为何如此快？一份技术拆解</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 12:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 375</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 170</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48437609'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into Linear&#x27;s performance, explaining how the product achieves snappy UX from API design to frontend optimizations. The breakdown covers efficient data fetching, caching, optimistic UI, and deployment choices, with takeaways for building fast SaaS apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文从 API 设计到前端优化，解析 Linear 如何实现流畅的用户体验。重点包括高效数据获取、缓存、乐观 UI 和基础设施部署等要点，以及对构建快速 SaaS 应用的可借鉴之处。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48395938' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48395938' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aoav.org.uk/2026/military-experts-or-arms-industry-insiders-uk-media-fails-to-disclose-defence-sector-links-in-nearly-60-of-cases/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UK media fails to disclose defence sector links in nearly 60% of cases</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国媒体在近60%的报道中未披露防务领域关联</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 01:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48395938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 374</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48395938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 212</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48395938'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis finds UK media frequently omits defence-sector links among the sources they cite, with disclosure missing in almost 60% of cases. The lack of transparency raises questions about conflicts of interest shaping coverage on arms, policy debates, and public trust in journalism. Strengthening disclosure norms could help readers better assess credibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项分析显示，英国媒体在报道中经常未披露与防务行业相关的利益关系，近60%的案例缺乏披露。这种透明度不足可能影响军备议题报道的客观性并削弱公众对新闻的信任。完善披露标准有助于读者更准确评估报道可信度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48345248' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48345248' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simpleflying.com/united-airlines-767-returns-newark-bluetooth-name-alert/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>United Airlines 767 returns to Newark after Bluetooth name sparks alert</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>联合航空 767 因蓝牙名称触发警报返航纽瓦克</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 05:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48345248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 369</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48345248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 732</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48345248'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A United Airlines 767 diverted to Newark after a Bluetooth name triggered an alert, prompting security protocols to kick in. The incident underscores how small device-level identifiers can influence aviation safety and the challenges of pervasive Bluetooth in cabins.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一架联合航空 767 因蓝牙名称触发警报而返航纽瓦克，启动安保程序。事件凸显机舱内的蓝牙设备标识可能对航空安全产生影响，以及在舱内广泛使用蓝牙所带来的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static0.simpleflyingimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/770-united-airlines-boeing-767-400-alexandre-rotenberg-_-shutterstock.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://static0.simpleflyingimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/770-united-airlines-boeing-767-400-alexandre-rotenberg-_-shutterstock.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48363132' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48363132' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/openai-frontier-models-and-codex-are-now-available-on-aws/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI frontier models and Codex are now available on AWS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI Frontier 模型与 Codex 现已在 AWS 上线</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 14:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48363132' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 363</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48363132' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48363132'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI frontier models and Codex are now available on AWS, enabling developers to deploy large-scale AI capabilities within their existing AWS workflows. The integration supports governance, data locality, and scalable deployment, catalyzing AI adoption for enterprises already in the AWS ecosystem. It signals a cloud-first distribution pattern for OpenAI&#x27;s evolving offerings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 的 Frontier 模型与 Codex 现已在 AWS 可用，使开发者能够在现有的 AWS 工作流中部署大规模 AI 能力。该整合支持企业级治理、数据本地化与可扩展部署，可能加速已在 AWS 生态中的企业采用 AI。此举也体现 OpenAI 持续以云端分发新产品的策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/2Tbsus4QAtPDAlsIM3vYO2/87f4c6894832d7937e54ecb3c245fdbe/OAI_AWS_Partnership_16x9_Art_Card.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/2Tbsus4QAtPDAlsIM3vYO2/87f4c6894832d7937e54ecb3c245fdbe/OAI_AWS_Partnership_16x9_Art_Card.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48414027' data-tags='Programming,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48414027' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sumnerevans.com/posts/software-engineering/stop-using-conventional-commits/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Conventional Commits encourages focus on the wrong things</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Conventional Commits 将关注点放在错误的地方</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 08:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48414027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 363</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48414027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 265</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48414027'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique argues Conventional Commits focuses on commit message formatting rather than meaningful changes, potentially harming review quality and tooling. It suggests rebalancing emphasis toward semantic commits and broader health signals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Conventional Commits 的批评认为它把注意力放在提交信息格式上，而忽略了变更的语义与代码质量，可能削弱审查和工具链的效果。建议将关注点转向更有意义的变更和项目健康信号。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sumnerevans.com/profile_hu_ec6b45997520a46d.webp' data-full='https://sumnerevans.com/profile_hu_ec6b45997520a46d.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48385965' data-tags='Hardware,AI,Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48385965' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.espressif.com/en/products/socs/esp32-s31' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ESP32-S31</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ESP32-S31 芯片</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 09:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48385965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 357</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48385965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 197</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48385965'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Espressif&#x27;s ESP32-S31 expands the low-power IoT SoC family with a focus on security and edge ML. The platform aims to deliver more secure, capable microcontrollers for smart devices, sensors, and wearables, broadening opportunities for on-device AI and encryption. As edge computing grows, S31 chips may accelerate privacy-preserving smart products.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Espressif 推出 ESP32-S31，扩展低功耗物联网系统芯片家族，强调安全性并支持边缘机器学习。该平台为智能设备、传感器和穿戴设备提供更安全、功能更强的微控制器，推动就地 AI 与加密能力。随着边缘计算兴起，S31 可能加速隐私保护型智能产品的落地。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.espressif.com/sites/all/themes/espressif/logo-black.svg' data-full='https://www.espressif.com/sites/all/themes/espressif/logo-black.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48400588' data-tags='Education,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48400588' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://havenweb.org/2026/05/28/retro-tech.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Retro-Tech Parenting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>复古科技育儿</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 09:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48400588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 341</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48400588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 238</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48400588'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Retro technology is examined in the context of parenting, showing how older devices influence kids’ tech literacy and family routines. The piece weighs nostalgia against practicality as families reintroduce vintage gear into everyday life.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在育儿语境下，讨论复古科技如何影响孩子的科技素养与家庭日常。文中权衡怀旧与实用性，探讨在日常生活中重新使用老式设备的利弊。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48373391' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48373391' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techradar.com/pro/quote-of-the-day-by-oracle-co-founder-larry-ellison-citizens-will-be-on-their-best-behavior-because-were-constantly-recording-and-reporting-everything-that-is-going-on-a-dire-warning-on-the-erosion-of-privacy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Larry Ellison: &quot;Citizens will be on their best behavior because we’re recording&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>拉里·埃利森：‘因为我们在记录，公民将行为最端正’</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 10:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48373391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 340</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48373391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 262</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48373391'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Larry Ellison argues that constant recording will push citizens to behave better, framing surveillance as governance. The remark spotlights privacy trade-offs that tech platforms may need to navigate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>埃利森表示持续记录会让公民表现得更好，把监控视为治理工具。这一说法凸显了科技平台在隐私与安全之间需要权衡的取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/46W7Uv8dFoPk5h4zPBEJqa-2560-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/46W7Uv8dFoPk5h4zPBEJqa-2560-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48423762' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pokeemerald.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pokemon Emerald Ported to WebAssembly (100k FPS)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>口袋妖怪绿宝石移植到 WebAssembly，帧率破万</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 04:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48423762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 339</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48423762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48423762'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Porting Pokemon Emerald to WebAssembly demonstrates how WebAssembly can run performance-sensitive emulations in the browser at high frame rates. The project highlights the potential for retro-game experiences with near-native speed and raises questions about licensing, compatibility, and toolchains for WASM-based ports.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将口袋妖怪绿宝石移植到 WebAssembly，表明网页端的高性能仿真有望实现接近原生的运行速度。该项目展示了复古游戏在浏览器中的潜力，同时引发对授权、兼容性与 WASM 移植工具链的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48425528' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48425528' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/SubscriberLink/1076018/16f01bbbb8e0d1f0/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Moving beyond fork() + exec()</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>跳出 fork() 与 exec() 的进程创建方法</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 07:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425528' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 338</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425528' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 324</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48425528'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Traditional fork/exec models remain standard but incur significant overhead and security risks. The discussion surveys modern alternatives for spawning processes, such as safer APIs, reduced duplication, and improved sandboxing. For developers, the takeaway is to rethink process creation strategy in runtime design and portability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>传统的 fork/exec 模型仍然常用，但开销大且存在安全风险。文章考察了更现代的进程创建替代方案，如更安全的 API、减少重复创建以及改进沙箱化。开发者应重新评估运行时设计中的进程创建策略与可移植性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48382142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 332</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48382142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48382142'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PlayStation architecture deep dive reveals Sony’s hardware choices across CPU, GPU, memory, and IO, with implications for game performance, dev tooling, and platform longevity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PlayStation 架构的深入解析揭示了索尼在 CPU/GPU、内存和 I/O 等方面的硬件取舍，对游戏性能、开发工具链和平台生命周期有重要影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48360054' data-tags='Data,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48360054' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://30fps.net/pages/255-vs-256-division/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Should you normalize RGB values by 255 or 256?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RGB 值应除以 255 还是 256？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 10:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48360054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 328</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48360054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48360054'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains the math behind normalizing 0–255 color channels to a 0–1 range and why dividing by 255 is usually preferable to 256. Highlights practical implications for image processing, neural networks, and color accuracy, including edge-case behavior at 255.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解释将 0–255 的颜色通道归一化到 0–1 的数学原理，以及为何通常应使用 255 而非 256 进行除法。强调在图像处理、神经网络与色彩精度方面的实际影响，特别是对 255 的端点处理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://30fps.net/pages/255-vs-256-division/std_vs_alt_numberline.png' data-full='https://30fps.net/pages/255-vs-256-division/std_vs_alt_numberline.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48349487' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48349487' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.promptarmor.com/resources/gpt-for-google-sheets-data-exfiltration' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ChatGPT for Google Sheets exfiltrates workbooks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google 表格的 ChatGPT 插件可能外泄工作簿数据</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 13:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48349487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 322</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48349487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48349487'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Running ChatGPT inside Google Sheets can inadvertently expose workbook content to the model, creating data leakage risks. This demonstrates how AI copilots can unintentionally exfiltrate sensitive data via prompts and interactions. It underscores the need for strict data governance and prompt-design safeguards when embedding AI in productivity tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Google 表格中运行 ChatGPT 可能无意间将工作簿内容暴露给模型，带来数据外泄风险。这揭示了 AI 辅助工具在提示与交互中可能无意地外带敏感数据。强调在把 AI 嵌入生产力工具时，需加强数据治理与提示设计的防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://framerusercontent.com/images/eJKU8yDyAKTNNHfYvQqWDm5e25s.png?width=6679&amp;height=4516' data-full='https://framerusercontent.com/images/eJKU8yDyAKTNNHfYvQqWDm5e25s.png?width=6679&amp;height=4516' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48403588' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.buchodi.com/meta-glasses-facial-recognition/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta&#x27;s ships facial recognition on smart glasses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 的智能眼镜搭载人脸识别</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 12:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48403588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 322</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48403588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 293</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48403588'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta is shipping facial recognition capabilities on smart glasses, raising privacy and safety concerns about real-time person recognition in public spaces. The move has regulatory and ethical implications for wearables and social media.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 在智能眼镜上引入人脸识别功能，引发对隐私和监控的担忧。此举对可穿戴设备的合规性、伦理问题以及公共场域的使用场景提出挑战。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48385114' data-tags='Security,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48385114' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://letsencrypt.org/2026/06/03/pq-certs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Post-Quantum Future for Let&#x27;s Encrypt</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt 的后量子时代</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 08:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48385114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 317</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48385114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48385114'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt outlines a plan for a post-quantum TLS ecosystem, including preparing for quantum-resistant certificates and hybrid crypto approaches. The move reflects the broader industry shift toward quantum-safe web security and the need for gradual crypto migrations without breaking existing sites.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Let’s Encrypt 发布后量子时代路线图，计划为 TLS 引入抗量子证书并采用混合加密策略。此举反映了行业向量子安全的转型，以及在不破坏现有网站的前提下逐步完成加密迁移的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://letsencrypt.org/images/LetsEncrypt-SocialShare.png' data-full='https://letsencrypt.org/images/LetsEncrypt-SocialShare.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48424605' data-tags='Hardware,AI,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48424605' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/lemire/status/2062880075117113739' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia is proposing a beast of a CPU system for Windows PCs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NVIDIA 为 Windows PC 提出一款强悍的 CPU 系统</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 05:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48424605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 314</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48424605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 504</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48424605'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA proposes a high-performance CPU system for Windows PCs, signaling a move beyond GPUs into desktop silicon. The concept hints at deep integration with Nvidia accelerators and software stacks, potentially boosting gaming, AI workloads, and content creation while challenging incumbents. If Windows compatibility and driver support align, it could reshape the PC hardware landscape.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英伟达提出面向 Windows PC 的高性能 CPU 系统，标志着其在桌面硬件领域向 GPU 之外扩展的野心。该设想或将深度整合其加速器和软件生态，提升游戏、AI 与创作工作负载的性能，同时对现有厂商形成挑战。如若 Windows 兼容性和驱动生态配套到位，市场格局或将被重新塑造。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jf.x.com/images/media-preview/2062880075117113739' data-full='https://jf.x.com/images/media-preview/2062880075117113739' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48375393' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48375393' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.acdw.net/clojure/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My thoughts after using Clojure for about a month</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用了一个月 Clojure 的心得</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 12:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48375393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 313</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48375393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 184</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48375393'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After a month using Clojure, the author notes a productive REPL-driven workflow and powerful macro system, but warns about onboarding friction for teams and the Java interop pitfalls. Clojure shines for concise, data-driven code yet demands discipline to maintain readability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者用一个月的时间体验 Clojure，认为 REPL 驱动的工作流高效、宏系统强大，但对团队协作和 Java 互操作的学习成本需谨慎。Clojure 在数据驱动、简洁代码方面表现突出，但要保持代码可维护性需自律。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 04:16 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lathe uses LLMs to thoroughly learn a new domain rather than skimming it, enabling deeper understanding and reproducible workflows. This approach highlights how AI can augment genuine knowledge acquisition, not just search or prompt tailoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lathe 通过大模型深入学习一个新领域，而不是走马观花，从而实现更深的理解和可重复的工作流。这显示了 AI 能力在真正的知识获取方面的潜力，而不仅仅是搜索或提示微调。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/05/27/meta-officially-launches-instagram-facebook-and-whatsapp-subscriptions-with-more-to-come-including-ai-plans/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta launches Instagram, Facebook, and WhatsApp subscriptions</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 10:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48347354' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 286</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48347354' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 503</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48347354'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta officially rolls out paid subscriptions across its main apps, signaling a shift toward creator monetization and premium experiences. Coupled with forthcoming AI features, the move hints at how social platforms may balance revenue, user value, and data use going forward.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 正式在旗下应用推出付费订阅，标志着向创作者变现和更高端体验的转型。再加上未来的 AI 功能，这一举措暗示了社交平台在未来如何平衡收入、用户价值与数据使用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48413295' data-tags='Politics,Privacy,Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48413295' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nltimes.nl/2026/06/05/dutch-govt-will-allow-european-company-operate-digid-platform' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dutch gov&#x27;t will only allow European company to operate DigiD platform</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>荷兰政府将仅允许欧洲公司运营 DigiD 平台</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 07:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48413295' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 285</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48413295' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48413295'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Netherlands will restrict DigiD platform operation to a European company, tightening control over a critical digital identity service. The move aims to enhance data sovereignty, security, and compliance with EU privacy norms, possibly in response to concerns about foreign ownership. For tech vendors and public-sector teams, it signals tougher procurement and vendor eligibility criteria for national infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>荷兰将 DigiD 平台的运营权限定为欧洲公司，增强关键数字身份服务的主权和安全性。此举意在提升数据所在地区的控制力和合规性，或回应对外国所有权的担忧。对技术供应商和公共部门团队而言，意味着国家基础设施的采购和准入标准将更加严格。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nltimes.nl/sites/nltimes.nl/files/styles/news_article_full_desktop_2x/public/2026-01/DigiD_app_on_a_Google_Pixel_4A%2C_Hillegersberg%2C_Rotterdam_%282024%29_01.jpg?h=5631aae5' data-full='https://nltimes.nl/sites/nltimes.nl/files/styles/news_article_full_desktop_2x/public/2026-01/DigiD_app_on_a_Google_Pixel_4A%2C_Hillegersberg%2C_Rotterdam_%282024%29_01.jpg?h=5631aae5' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

The takeaway is a push for rigorous math to back reliable AI deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数学家警告人工智能的进展正在快速超出现有理论与验证手段的能力。文章呼吁在 AI 安全、形式化验证和治理方面建立更扎实的数学基础，以应对大规模模型带来的新挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.scotthyoung.com/blog/2026/05/27/revolutionize-schooling/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m skeptical about efforts to revolutionize schooling</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我对改革教育的努力持怀疑态度</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 13:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48376008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 267</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48376008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 430</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48376008'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scott Young questions the hype around tech-driven schooling reforms, arguing that real progress requires evidence-based, scalable changes rather than flashy interventions. He emphasizes fundamentals like pedagogy, assessment, and equitable access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者对以科技手段彻底改造教育的热潮持怀疑态度，主张以证据为基础推进改革。强调改革应着眼于课程、师资培训、教育公平等根本问题，而非追逐新潮工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.scotthyoung.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Revolutionizing-education.jpg' data-full='https://www.scotthyoung.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Revolutionizing-education.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48358667' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.politico.com/news/2026/06/01/openai-hit-with-florida-lawsuit-00944215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Florida sues OpenAI and Sam Altman over AI risks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>佛州就AI风险起诉OpenAI及Altman</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 09:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48358667' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 266</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48358667' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 194</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48358667'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Florida filed a lawsuit against OpenAI and CEO Sam Altman alleging that the company&#x27;s AI products pose risks and misrepresent safety measures. The case adds to growing regulatory scrutiny of AI technologies and could influence how consumer-protection standards are enforced for AI services. The outcome may set a precedent for accountability in AI safety claims.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>佛州针对OpenAI及首席执行官萨姆·奥特曼提起诉讼，指控其AI产品存在风险并误导安全承诺。此案反映出日益严厉的AI监管态势，可能影响对AI服务的消费者保护标准执行，并或为AI安全承诺设定先例。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48355720' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.windowslatest.com/2026/06/01/microsoft-builds-its-ultimate-macbook-pro-rival-with-the-nvidia-powered-surface-laptop-ultra/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft builds MacBook Pro rival with NVIDIA-powered Surface Laptop Ultra</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软打造搭载 NVIDIA 的 Surface Laptop Ultra，欲挑战 MacBook Pro</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 05:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48355720' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 265</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48355720' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 553</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48355720'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft is developing a premium Surface Laptop Ultra powered by NVIDIA GPUs to compete with the MacBook Pro. The project signals a stronger emphasis on high-end laptops for professional workloads, including graphics and AI-assisted tasks. If successful, it could shift hardware competitiveness in the Windows ecosystem toward creator-focused configurations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软正在开发搭载 NVIDIA 显卡的高端 Surface Laptop Ultra，以与 MacBook Pro 竞争。该计划凸显对专业工作负载的高端笔记本需求，涵盖图形和 AI 辅助任务。若成型，将可能提升 Windows 生态在面向创作者的硬件配置方面的竞争力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.windowslatest.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Microsoft-announces-the-Surface-Laptop-Ultra-powered-by-NVIDIA-RTX-SPARK.jpg' data-full='https://www.windowslatest.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Microsoft-announces-the-Surface-Laptop-Ultra-powered-by-NVIDIA-RTX-SPARK.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48357905' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48357905' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/the-dirt-that-refused-to-die-20260601/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What appear to be biochemical processes may be a natural feature of geology</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>看似生化过程其实是地质的自然特征</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 08:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48357905' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 260</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48357905' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48357905'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers argue that some biochemical-like processes can arise from geological conditions, complicating interpretations of life signals. The finding urges caution when distinguishing biotic versus abiotic signals in geochemistry and astrobiology, with implications for how we search for life on other worlds. This work underscores the need for robust, multi-faceted evidence when assessing potential biosignatures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者认为某些看似生物的化学过程其实来自地质条件，这使对生命信号的解读变得更复杂。在地球化学和天体生物学中区分生物信号与非生物信号需要更谨慎，这对在其他世界寻找生命痕迹具有重要影响。该研究强调在评估潜在生物标志时需依赖多层次证据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Abiotic-metabolism-cr-Samuel-Velasco-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Abiotic-metabolism-cr-Samuel-Velasco-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48431461' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ValveSoftware/GameNetworkingSockets/issues/398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Major P2P issues in Israel and possibly other Middle East countries</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以色列及其他中东地区的主要P2P问题</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 20:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48431461' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 260</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48431461' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48431461'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It underscores the fragility of P2P under restrictive networks and the need for fallback strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中讨论了以色列及其他中东地区的实际点对点网络问题，如 NAT 穿透、对称性防火墙与连通性限制，影响多人游戏等 P2P 应用的表现。强调在受限网络环境下 P2P 的脆弱性，并提出需要回退策略与改进的连接方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c2d71f3d86cfcf2495c70dc0db549513166ac8cf7d38f991af1e7872e1a43ff4/ValveSoftware/GameNetworkingSockets/issues/398' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c2d71f3d86cfcf2495c70dc0db549513166ac8cf7d38f991af1e7872e1a43ff4/ValveSoftware/GameNetworkingSockets/issues/398' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每一个字节都很重要</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在现代系统中，每一个字节都至关重要，关系到延迟、能耗、隐私和成本。文章主张通过更高效的数据实践获得竞争优势。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48425723' data-tags='Open Source,Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48425723' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://su3.io/posts/introducing-zeroserve' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zeroserve: A zero-config web server you can script with eBPF</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zeroserve：一个零配置、可用 eBPF 脚本化的 Web 服务器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 07:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425723' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 257</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425723' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48425723'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zeroserve is a zero-config web server you can script with eBPF. It lets you inject custom behavior using eBPF programs, enabling dynamic routing, metrics, and security with low overhead. This approach could lower the barrier to deploying flexible, observable services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zeroserve 是一个零配置的 Web 服务器，支持通过 eBPF 脚本化自定义行为。借助内核级的可编程能力，它可在边缘进行流量路由、监控和访问控制等定制，而无需繁琐的配置。这样的设计降低了部署动态、可观测服务的门槛，提升灵活性和性能边界。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/krauseler/muxcard' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Muxcard, a DIY credit card size computer</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48342399' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48342399' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Muxcard is a DIY, credit-card-sized computer project that aims to be a portable, hackable development board. It emphasizes compact form factor while offering entry-level tooling and expandability for hobbyists. The project fits into the ongoing trend of pocketable computing devices for learning and prototype hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Muxcard 是一个自制的信用卡大小的计算机项目，旨在提供便携且可黑客化的开发板。其在紧凑体积的同时提供入门级的工具链与扩展性，面向爱好者进行学习与原型开发。这一设计体现了便携计算设备的持续热潮。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48406198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 254</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48406198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>South Korea considers mandating AI-based image censorship scans across online forums, raising concerns about privacy, censorship, and platform responsibility. The move could reshape how communities moderate content.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48374552' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://handwritten.danieljanus.pl/2026-06-01-edsger.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Edsger – A handwritten Clojure REPL for the reMarkable 2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：在 reMarkable 2 上运行的手写 Clojure REPL（Edsger）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 11:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48374552' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 252</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48374552' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48374552'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An experimental project ships a handwritten Clojure REPL on the reMarkable 2, letting you type and evaluate Clojure code directly on the e-ink device. It demonstrates how lightweight, on-device tooling can enable interactive programming far from a traditional IDE, even on constrained hardware. Proof-of-concept for embedding REPLs on unusual hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目把 Clojure 的手写 REPL 移植到 reMarkable 2，让在电子墨水设备上直接输入并执行代码成为可能。它展示了在资源受限设备上实现交互式编程的潜力，以及在极简硬件上的在地开发工具。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/06/scientists-ejected-from-diabetes-conference-for-distributing-journal-reprints/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scientists ejected from diabetes conference for distributing journal reprints</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 03:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48433410' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 252</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48433410' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 176</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48433410'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scientists were ejected from a diabetes conference for distributing journal reprints, highlighting tensions between open access and conference norms. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科学家因分发期刊重印件被糖尿病会议驱逐，暴露出开放获取与会议规范之间的紧张关系。此事凸显了研究可及性与版权、以及会议掌控之间的持续辩论。</p>
