<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48362515' data-tags='Finance,Business,AI,Hardware,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48362515' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://abc.xyz/investor/news/news-details/2026/Alphabet-Announces-Proposed-80-Billion-Equity-Capital-Raise-to-Expand-AI-Infrastructure-and-Compute-2026-b0myAMewCa/default.aspx' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alphabet announces $80B equity capital raise to expand AI infra and compute</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Alphabet宣布800亿美元股权融资以扩展AI基础设施与算力</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 13:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48362515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 251</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48362515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 231</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48362515'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alphabet plans to raise about $80B in equity to fund AI infrastructure and compute capacity, signaling continued scale-up of its AI platforms and data-center footprint. The move highlights the capital-intensive nature of modern AI at scale and could influence strategic investments across cloud and hardware ecosystems. Expect further expansion of Google&#x27;s AI tooling, services, and partnerships.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Alphabet计划筹集约800亿美元股本，以资助AI基础设施与算力扩展，显示其AI平台和数据中心规模化的持续增速。这一举措凸显大规模AI的高资本需求，可能影响云服务和硬件生态圈的战略投资。未来Google的AI工具、服务与合作关系预计将进一步扩张。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s206.q4cdn.com/479360582/files/design/alphabet_logo.png' data-full='https://s206.q4cdn.com/479360582/files/design/alphabet_logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48420148' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48420148' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://salvagedcircuitry.com/sigma-45mm.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The intracies of modern camera lens repair (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现代相机镜头维修的复杂性（2024）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 17:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48420148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 251</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48420148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48420148'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It delves into the hidden mechanics of repairing modern camera lenses—from tiny screws and tolerances to coatings and electronic interactions—that complicate field fixes. It also explains why repairability matters for sustainability and cost in 2024&#x27;s camera ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章深入探讨现代镜头修复的隐性机制，覆盖小螺丝、公差、涂层以及与传感器的微观交互等，解释这些因素为何让现场维修变得复杂。并指出修复能力对 2024 年相机生态的可持续性与成本的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://salvagedcircuitry.com/img/sigma-45mm/med/20240228_145227c.JPG' data-full='https://salvagedcircuitry.com/img/sigma-45mm/med/20240228_145227c.JPG' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48361064' data-tags='Legal,Open Source,Programming,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48361064' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eblog.fly.dev/githubbad.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub and the crime against software</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 与对软件的犯罪</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 11:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48361064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 248</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48361064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48361064'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that GitHub&#x27;s culture and tooling enable lax licensing and diffusion of copyrighted code, which the author calls a &#x27;crime against software.&#x27; The piece calls for clearer licenses, provenance tracking, and stronger attribution practices to restore incentives for proper software stewardship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>认为 GitHub 的文化与工具链放任许可宽松、作品代码的扩散，构成对软件的“犯罪行为”。呼吁更清晰的许可、源流跟踪与更强的署名规范，以恢复对软件所有权与治理的正确激励。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eblog.fly.dev/tt_tt.png' data-full='https://www.eblog.fly.dev/tt_tt.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48375175' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.zgp.org/the-advertising-cartel-coming-to-your-web-browser/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The advertising cartel coming to your web browser</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>广告寡头将进入你的网页浏览器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 12:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48375175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 244</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48375175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48375175'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative analysis argues that ad tech cartels are consolidating control at the browser level, raising concerns about privacy, interoperability, and user experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇分析指出广告科技寡头正在浏览器层面形成更强的垄断，带来隐私、互通性和用户体验方面的担忧。文章探讨浏览器厂商与监管者可能采取的对策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.zgp.org/i/rss.png' data-full='https://blog.zgp.org/i/rss.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kde.org/anniversaries/30/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>KDE at 30</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>KDE 三十周年</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 07:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48357355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 243</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48357355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48357355'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>KDE marks 30 years of open‑source desktop work, highlighting its impact on user interfaces, Linux ecosystems, and community‑driven software. The milestone invites reflection on sustainability and future directions for desktop environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>KDE 迎来三十周年，回顾开源桌面生态的影响、用户体验和社区驱动的软件发展。 milestone 也为未来的桌面环境方向带来反思。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48346019' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48346019' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://justine.lol/rseq/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Restartable Sequences</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可重启序列（Restartable Sequences）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 07:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48346019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 236</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48346019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48346019'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Restartable Sequences (rseq) is a Linux kernel feature that lets a thread execute a tiny critical section and restart safely if interrupted, reducing costly synchronization. The piece outlines how rseq enables fast-path code and where it fits in performance-sensitive apps, with caveats around portability and safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Restartable Sequences（rseq）是一个 Linux 内核特性，允许线程在被中断时安全地重启极小的临界区，从而降低同步成本。文章解释了 rseq 如何让快速路径代码更高效，以及在对性能敏感的应用中的适用场景，同时也提示了可移植性与安全性方面的注意。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://worker.jart.workers.dev/rseq/rseq.png' data-full='https://worker.jart.workers.dev/rseq/rseq.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 10:04 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48347370'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Deflock mapped 100,000 ALPR cameras across the United States, revealing distribution patterns and the scale of automated surveillance. The visualization raises questions about privacy, governance, and the reach of law enforcement tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Deflock 已将美国境内十万台 ALPR 摄像头的位置绘制成地图，呈现出区域分布与监控覆盖的规模。可视化数据引发对隐私、治理及执法技术影响的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.politico.com/news/2026/06/02/trump-signs-downsized-ai-order-00946389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trump signs downsized AI order after weeks of reversals</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特朗普在多周变动后签署缩减版 AI 命令</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 09:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48372628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 235</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48372628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48372628'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Trump signs a downsized AI order after weeks of reversals, signaling a pared-back but still strategic stance on AI governance. The move focuses on national security, workforce reskilling, and research priorities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特朗普在多轮改变后签署了一份缩减版的 AI 指令，尽管规模减小，但仍体现对 AI governance 的战略关注，聚焦国家安全、劳动力再培训与研究优先级。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48327453' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48327453' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fisheries.noaa.gov/feature-story/decades-effort-restore-steelhead-and-salmon-passage-californias-alameda-creek' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decades of Effort Restore Steelhead and Salmon Passage on Alameda Creek</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数十年努力重建阿拉梅达溪的钢头鲑鱼回游通道</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 29, 2026 / 11:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48327453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 231</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48327453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48327453'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Over decades, agencies and communities removed barriers and restored habitat to reopen steelhead and salmon migrations on California&#x27;s Alameda Creek. The effort improves biodiversity, supports endangered species recovery, and demonstrates scalable approaches for river restoration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数十年来，相关机构与社区清除阻碍、修复栖息地，使阿拉梅达溪的钢头鲑和鲑鱼能够重新回游。该工程提升了生物多样性，助力濒危物种恢复，并展示了可扩展的河流修复方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.fisheries.noaa.gov/s3//2026-05/2000x1333-leapingsteelheadadobestock.jpg' data-full='https://www.fisheries.noaa.gov/s3//2026-05/2000x1333-leapingsteelheadadobestock.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48413254' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48413254' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/leaders/2026/06/04/indias-surprise-baby-bust-is-a-warning-to-the-world' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>India&#x27;s surprise baby bust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>印度意外出生率下降</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 07:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48413254' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 231</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48413254' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 996</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48413254'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Economist reports India&#x27;s birth rate declined unexpectedly, signaling potential shifts in the demographic dividend and implications for labor supply, growth, and global population trends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>经济学人报道印度出生率意外下降，可能改变人口红利格局，对劳动力供给、经济增长及全球人口趋势带来影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48386551' data-tags='Politics,Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48386551' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sanders.senate.gov/op-eds/the-public-should-own-half-of-the-big-a-i-companies/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Public Should Own Half of the Big A.I. Companies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>公众应拥有大 AI 公司的一半股权</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 09:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48386551' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 230</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48386551' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 266</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48386551'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The op-ed argues that public ownership of half of the largest AI firms would curb concentration of power and ensure society captures a fair share of AI&#x27;s gains. It sketches mechanisms like public investment or governance rights to democratize control, and discusses potential risks and implementation challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇社论主张公众应持有大型 AI 公司的一半股权，以遏制权力集中并确保社会分享 AI 的收益。文中提出通过公共投资基金或治理权等机制实现公众参与，并讨论实施中的权衡与风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sanders.senate.gov/wp-content/uploads/2020/11/bernie-sanders-logo-greens-yellow-768x116.png' data-full='https://www.sanders.senate.gov/wp-content/uploads/2020/11/bernie-sanders-logo-greens-yellow-768x116.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48373054' data-tags='Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48373054' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jasonzweig.com/three-ways-to-get-paid/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Three Ways to Get Paid (2018)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>三种获得报酬的方式（2018）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 10:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48373054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 230</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48373054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48373054'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jason Zweig outlines three core ways people earn money: wages, investments, and owning income-producing assets. The piece blends practical finance tips with historical context to broaden how we think about compensation beyond salary.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者 Jason Zweig 指出三种核心赚钱方式：工资、投资，以及拥有带来收益的资产。文中将实用的理财要点与历史背景结合，帮助读者把工资以外的报酬纳入考量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jasonzweig.com/wp-content/uploads/2018/07/ForSale2.png' data-full='https://jasonzweig.com/wp-content/uploads/2018/07/ForSale2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48312443'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes experimenting with throttling performance to observe how devices behave under constrained resources. The piece offers insights into how throttling affects usability, battery life, and privacy considerations.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discourse.julialang.org/t/pluto-1-0-release/137296' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pluto.jl 1.0 release – reactive notebook for Julia</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 16:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48377496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48377496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48377496'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pluto.jl 1.0 delivers a reactive notebook for Julia with dependency-aware execution and reproducibility. The release lowers the barrier to interactive data exploration and teaching in Julia.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pluto.jl 1.0 推出，提供面向 Julia 的完全反应式笔记本，具备对依赖关系的自动跟踪与可重复执行性。该版本降低了交互式数据探索的门槛，便于教学与研究使用。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://uruky.com/?il=en' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Uruky (EU-based Kagi alternative) now has Image Search and URL Rewrites</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Uruky——面向欧盟的 Kagi 替代，现已支持图片搜索与 URL 重写</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 01:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48396004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48396004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 213</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48396004'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Uruky, a EU-based alternative to Kagi, introduced Image Search and URL Rewrite features, expanding its feature set as a privacy-focused search option. The release highlights how EU-based independents continue to compete with larger incumbents by adding more useful tooling for developers and power users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Uruky 作为基于欧盟的 Kagi 替代，新增图片搜索与 URL 重写功能，扩展了其隐私导向搜索的功能集。此次更新显示，基于欧盟的独立搜索引擎正通过增加实用工具来与大型对手竞争，服务开发者与高阶用户。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48392082' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48392082' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/engineering/how-we-contain-claude' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The ways we contain Claude across products</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们在各产品中对 Claude 的 containment 方法</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 17:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48392082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 223</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48392082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48392082'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Across products, we implement multiple containment layers for Claude: guardrails, input filtering, rate limits, and a policy-driven oversight model. The goal is to prevent unsafe outputs while preserving useful capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在各产品中对 Claude 的 containment 包括多层安全策略、输入输出过滤、速率限流与策略治理等。目标是在保留有用功能的同时尽量避免不安全输出。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/82d2262129af025d98a46411fbd42ee970a95cb4-2400x1260.heif' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/82d2262129af025d98a46411fbd42ee970a95cb4-2400x1260.heif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Coreutils for Windows brings GNU utilities like ls and cp to Windows native, streamlining cross-platform scripting without relying on WSL or Cygwin.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Odysseus provides a self-hosted AI workspace for teams to manage models and experiments locally, emphasizing privacy, reproducibility, and control. It appeals to developers seeking to avoid vendor lock-in and data leaving their environment.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lemire.me/blog/2026/05/22/only-17-of-all-64-bit-integers-are-products-of-two-32-bit-integers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Only 17% of all 64-bit Integers are products of two 32-bit integers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>只有 64 位整数中有 17% 可以分解为两个 32 位整数的乘积</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 28, 2026 / 09:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48311003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 219</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48311003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48311003'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Presents a number-theory result: only about 17% of 64-bit integers admit a factorization into two 32-bit factors; discusses consequences for algorithm design, randomization, and cryptography.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>给出一个数论结果：约 17% 的 64 位整数可以分解为两个 32 位因子的乘积；讨论对算法设计、随机化以及密码学的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://variety.com/2026/film/box-office/backrooms-box-office-record-opening-weekend-obsession-jumps-star-wars-crumbles-1236763355/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Backrooms&#x27; Stuns with $81M Debut</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《Backrooms》凭借8100万美元的首映票房，显示强劲市场</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 12:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48348864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 218</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48348864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 169</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48348864'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Backrooms opens with $81M, signaling a strong debut for a mid‑budget horror title. The result underscores continued appetite for theatrical releases and IP‑driven momentum even as streaming competes for attention.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Backrooms》凭借8100万美元的首映票房，显示中等预算恐怖片仍具吸引力。说明在流媒体竞争激烈的环境下，影院上映仍能带来强劲的市场回报，尤其在知名 IP 的带动下。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球食品体系中的超加工食品：烟草公司的角色</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48411190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 216</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48411190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 285</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48411190'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece analyzes how tobacco companies increasingly participate in ultra-processed foods to diversify revenue. It traces marketing tactics, lobbying, and regulatory vulnerabilities, underscoring public health implications and the need for tighter oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章分析烟草公司如何通过进入超加工食品领域多元化收入来源，追踪其营销手段、游说活动以及监管漏洞，强调对公众健康的影响并呼吁加强监管。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48405931' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48405931' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04032' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Do transformers need three projections? Systematic study of QKV variants</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Transformer 是否需要三重投影？对 QKV 变体的系统研究</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 16:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48405931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 215</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48405931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48405931'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The study systematically compares QKV variants to see whether transformers truly require three separate projections. Results illuminate whether shared projections can match accuracy with less compute, impacting design choices for future models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该论文系统性比较了 QKV 变体，探讨变换器是否真的需要三个独立的投影。结果为未来模型在准确性与计算成本之间的权衡提供实证依据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48428763' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.randalolson.com/2026/06/04/recent-grad-unemployment-flip/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New U.S. college grads now have higher unemployment than the average worker</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新近美国高校毕业生的失业率现在高于全国平均水平</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 13:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48428763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48428763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 255</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48428763'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New graduates are facing unemployment rates higher than the overall average, underscoring a softer entry-level job market and persistent macro headwinds. This trend could temper early-career hiring, salary growth, and the overall pace of tech industry onboarding for the next wave of graduates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新毕业生的失业率高于全国平均水平，体现入门级就业市场疲软和宏观因素的持续影响。这一趋势可能抑制科技行业的初期招聘、薪资增长以及新一代毕业生的职业起步速度。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48373909' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 211</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48373909' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Morningstar pegs SpaceX at about $780B, roughly half its rumored IPO target, illustrating how private market enthusiasm and public-market expectations diverge. The valuation signals investor fever for space tech even as the company remains private.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48382047' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://redis.io/blog/announcing-redis-8-8/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Redis 8.8: New array data structure, rate limiter, performance improvements</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Redis 8.8：新增数组结构、速率限制器与性能提升</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 03:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48382047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 211</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48382047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48382047'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Redis 8.8 ships with a new array data structure, a built-in rate limiter, and various performance improvements. These changes simplify data modeling, improve traffic control, and boost overall throughput for apps relying on Redis.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Redis 8.8 发布，新增数组数据结构、内置速率限制器，以及多项性能改进。这些改动简化数据建模、提升流控能力，并提高对 Redis 依赖的应用的整体吞吐。</p>

The rest of us paid $400M</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿瑟顿花费 14.5 万美元拖延 Caltrain 电气化，其余人花费 4 亿美元并多等 3 年</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 14:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48350131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48350131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48350131'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A local political decision reveals how small-budget lobbying can delay infrastructure electrification, shifting costs to the broader public: a $400M hit and three more years of delay. The piece shows how local decision-making can influence tech adoption and climate goals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>地方政治人物花费14.5万美元拖延 Caltrain 电气化，而大众则承担约4亿美元的成本并多等3年。这个案例揭示了地方决策如何影响技术普及与气候目标的实现。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48374685' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48374685' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hpcalcs.com/product/hp-16c-collectors-edition/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HP re-releases classic computer science calculator: The HP-16C</a></p>

<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kristoff.it/blog/north-star/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My Software North Star</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48389368'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise personal manifesto about the single North Star guiding software decisions: simplicity, reliability, and user value. The post argues that a single guiding principle helps steer architecture, tooling, and process choices toward durable software results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者提出一个关于软件开发的单一北极星原则，强调以简单性、可靠性和对用户价值的关注为核心。文章解释了如何将这一原则应用到架构、工具选择和工作流程中，以实现更持久的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48373642' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48373642' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/U.S._Army_Corps_of_Engineers_Bay_Model' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. Army Corps of Engineers Bay Model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国陆军工程兵团海湾模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 10:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48373642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 200</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48373642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48373642'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Bay Model is a large-scale physical hydraulic model built by the U.S. Army Corps of Engineers to simulate tides, currents, and flood scenarios for San Francisco Bay. It represents mid-20th-century physical modeling that complemented early digital tools in coastal engineering and planning, influencing how engineers study hydrodynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国陆军工程兵团用来模拟圣弗朗西斯科湾潮汐、水流与洪水情景的大型物理水动力模型，体现20世纪中叶物理建模在工程决策中的作用。它帮助研究沿海管理、港湾开发和灾害预测的直观理解，并推动了后续数字仿真的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/87/USCAE_Bay_Model_-_San_Francisco_Bay_Detail.jpg/1280px-USCAE_Bay_Model_-_San_Francisco_Bay_Detail.jpg' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/87/USCAE_Bay_Model_-_San_Francisco_Bay_Detail.jpg/1280px-USCAE_Bay_Model_-_San_Francisco_Bay_Detail.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48309492' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48309492' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/security/2026/05/websites-have-a-new-way-to-spy-on-visitors-analyzing-their-ssd-activity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Websites have a new way to spy on visitors: analyzing their SSD activity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>网站新招隐形监视：分析 SSD 活动以窥探访客</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 28, 2026 / 07:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48309492' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 197</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48309492' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48309492'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers show websites can glean visitor info by analyzing SSD IO patterns, a surprising and invasive data leakage vector. It motivates stricter browser and OS controls to reduce micro-timing exposures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员展示网站可以通过分析 SSD IO 模式来推断访客信息，成为一种令人意外且具侵入性的数据泄露途径。促使浏览器和系统加强对微时序泄露的防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/solid-state-drive-ssd-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/solid-state-drive-ssd-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48347153' data-tags='AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48347153' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://darylcecile.net/notes/speed-of-prototyping-age-of-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Speed of Prototyping in the Age of AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI时代的原型设计速度</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 09:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48347153' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 196</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48347153' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48347153'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI accelerates prototyping by automating design, code, and testing loops, letting teams iterate ideas faster. The flip side is higher risks of tech debt and quality gaps, so teams should pair speed with guardrails like clear requirements and automated testing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 让原型设计循环更加快速，自动化设计、编码和测试，帮助团队更快将想法落地。与此同时，追求速度也可能带来技术债务和质量隐患，因此需要用清晰需求和自动化测试等防护措施来平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://darylcecile.net/og?slug=speed-of-prototyping-age-of-ai' data-full='https://darylcecile.net/og?slug=speed-of-prototyping-age-of-ai' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/duanebester/gooey' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gooey: A GPU-accelerated UI framework for Zig</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gooey：用于 Zig 的 GPU 加速 UI 框架</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 10:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48386725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 196</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48386725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48386725'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gooey is a GPU-accelerated UI framework for Zig, offering high-performance rendering while staying language-friendly. It signals growing interest in GPU-accelerated UI toolchains outside mainstream JS frameworks and could enable more responsive, graphics-heavy apps built in Zig.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gooey 是面向 Zig 的 GPU 加速 UI 框架，提供高性能渲染并保持语言友好性。它体现了在非主流语言生态中对 GPU 加速 UI 工具链的兴趣，或推动用 Zig 构建更流畅、图形密集型的应用。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces ESP32 Bit Pirate, a hardware hacking toolkit with a WebCLI capable of talking multiple protocols. It lowers barriers for hardware testing and security research, while highlighting ethical and safety considerations.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48326721' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48326721' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/2205e2d0-50dc-4e80-9bf7-78d0272276c0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What if remote working, not AI, is to blame for weak junior hiring?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>若是远程工作，而非 AI，成为初级招聘乏力的原因？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 29, 2026 / 10:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48326721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48326721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 253</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48326721'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FT piece argues that remote work dynamics—hiring pipelines, wage inflation, and distributed interviewing—may be more responsible for weak junior hiring than AI automation. It explores implications for recruiters and policy on training, compensation, and onboarding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>金融时报的文章认为，远程工作带来的招聘管道、薪资膨胀和分布式面试等因素，可能比 AI 自动化更致力于削弱初级岗位招聘。内容探讨对招聘、培训、薪酬和入职流程的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2Fbbbb7bb6-d71b-49e6-8ae0-bb08c48ecc91.jpg?source=next-barrier-page' data-full='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2Fbbbb7bb6-d71b-49e6-8ae0-bb08c48ecc91.jpg?source=next-barrier-page' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lucasfcosta.com/blog/backpressure-is-all-you-need' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Backpressure is all you need</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>回压才是王道</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 05:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48345090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 192</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48345090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48345090'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Backpressure is all you need argues that backpressure is the essential principle for building robust streaming systems, preventing memory pressure spikes and cascading failures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回压才是王道 主张回压是构建健壮流式系统的关键原则，能防止内存压力激增和级联故障。文中给出跨语言的实现模式与实用示例，帮助工程师在生产中落地回压。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 03:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48396792' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 188</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48396792' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48396792'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gaussian Point Splatting is a rendering technique that uses Gaussian kernels to render point clouds with high fidelity and efficiency. The approach promises faster rendering and lower memory usage for complex scenes, with potential impact on real-time graphics and volume rendering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了高斯点溅射渲染，这种渲染技术用高斯核来高保真地呈现点云，兼具高效性。该方法有望提升复杂场景的渲染速度并降低显存需求，对实时图形和体绘制具有潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://momentsingraphics.de/Media/Siggraph2026/representative_image.webp' data-full='https://momentsingraphics.de/Media/Siggraph2026/representative_image.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48408442' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://passo.uno/fine-tuning-docs-llm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fine-tuning an LLM to write docs like it&#x27;s 1995</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让 LLM 写出1995年的文档：微调</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 22:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48408442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 187</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48408442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48408442'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look at fine-tuning a large language model to reproduce the tone and conventions of 1990s-era documentation. The approach raises questions about consistency, maintainability, and whether retro-styling aids comprehension for modern readers. The piece weighs benefits against potential outdated practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过对大型语言模型进行微调，使其输出1990年代风格的文档。该方法在提升风格一致性方面有潜在好处，但也可能带来术语落后与维护难题，并对现代读者的理解造成影响，需权衡取舍。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 03:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48344535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 183</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48344535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 197</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48344535'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A long-form investigation into money laundering, tracing how illicit funds move through opaque financial systems and the political economy behind enforcement gaps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对洗钱的规模和运作进行长篇调查，追踪不法资金如何在不透明的金融体系中流动，以及执法缺口背后的经济逻辑。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48411510' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48411510' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.plover.com/2026/03/09/#documentation-wins-2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Programmers will document for Claude, but not for each other</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>程序员会为 Claude 做文档化，而不是为同事编写文档</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 05:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48411510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 183</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48411510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48411510'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay argues that AI-assisted development changes the dynamics of documentation: developers should write docs as inputs for AI models like Claude, rather than just for human teammates. This shift could improve maintainability and onboarding as AI tools glean intent, but also raises questions about human-readable docs and team culture. The piece champions documentation practices that are robust for AI consumption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇文章认为 AI 助力的开发改变了文档的作用：程序员应为像 Claude 这样的 AI 模型编写文档，而不仅仅面向同事。此举有望提升可维护性和新员工的上手速度，但也带来对人类可读性文档和团队文化的挑战。文章倡导面向 AI 的稳健文档实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pic.blog.plover.com/FIRST_IMAGE_DEFAULT.jpg' data-full='https://pic.blog.plover.com/FIRST_IMAGE_DEFAULT.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48428356' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48428356' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.aec7671' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Home alone: Remote work, isolation, and mental health</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>独居时代的远程工作、孤立与心理健康</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 12:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48428356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 182</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48428356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 175</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48428356'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The study links prolonged remote work to increased isolation and mental health strain, with implications for productivity and retention. It highlights the importance of structured social interaction, mental health resources, and management practices as hybrid work becomes the norm.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现长期远程工作与孤独感和心理压力增加相关，可能影响生产力和员工留任。结果强调在混合工作成为常态时，建立有计划的社交互动、心理健康资源和管理实践的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48409675' data-tags='Science,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48409675' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/entanglement-builds-space-time-now-magic-gives-it-gravity-20260603/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Entanglement Builds Space-Time. Now &quot;Magic&quot; Gives It Gravity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纠缠构建时空，现在‘魔力’赋予引力</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 01:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48409675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 182</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48409675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 171</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48409675'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article explains how quantum entanglement may give rise to spacetime geometry and how new ideas attempt to treat gravity as an emergent phenomenon from quantum correlations. It highlights thought experiments and models linking entanglement, spacetime, and gravity, with implications for quantum gravity research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章解释量子纠缠如何可能产生时空几何，以及将引力视为量子相关性涌现效应的新理论思路。介绍了将纠缠、时空与引力联系起来的思路与模型，以及对量子引力研究的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Magic-Gravity-cr-Irene-Perez-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Magic-Gravity-cr-Irene-Perez-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://elijahpotter.dev/articles/hacker-news-sans-AI' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hacker News, Sans AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hacker News（无 AI）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 13:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48417916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 182</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48417916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48417916'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative look at what Hacker News would be like without AI augmentation. It argues about whether AI-assisted summaries and moderation actually improve signal quality, or if a more human-curated feed yields richer discussions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>思考如果 Hacker News 去除 AI 辅助会怎样。文章讨论 AI 辅助的摘要与审理是否真正提升信号质量，或者更偏好人工精挑细选的讨论带来更丰富的内容。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48440383' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48440383' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://teenage.engineering/products/apc-2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>APC–2 – A professional record cutter for producing original playback discs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>APC-2：专业级刻录机，制作原始播放唱片</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 18:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 181</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48440383'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>APC-2 is a professional vinyl cutter enabling artists to produce original playback discs, bypassing traditional mastering and pressing for small runs. It promises precise cuts and smoother workflow integration with modern audio tools, signaling a DIY resurgence in physical media. For independent artists, it lowers barriers to producing limited edition discs without large-scale facilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>APC-2 是一台专业级刻录机，使艺术家能够制作原创播放唱片，绕过传统的母带与压片流程以实现小批量生产。它承诺高精度切割和与现代音频工具的无缝对接，标志着实体媒体的DIY复兴。对于独立艺术家而言，这意味着无需大规模厂房也能发行限量版唱片。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.teenage.engineering/_img/6a22f2d3ab13747827da71c7_1024.webp' data-full='https://assets.teenage.engineering/_img/6a22f2d3ab13747827da71c7_1024.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48389284' data-tags='Culture,Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48389284' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lithub.com/the-man-who-reads-books-for-a-living-one-every-two-days/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Man Who Reads Books for a Living</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>靠读书谋生的人</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 13:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48389284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48389284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48389284'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A profile of a man who makes a living by reading, roughly one book every two days. It delves into the discipline, selection process, and culture of professional reading, and what that lifestyle reveals about attention, memory, and comprehension.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章聚焦一个以阅读谋生的人，每两天读一本书的节奏。它探讨了职业阅读的训练、筛选偏好与文化，以及这种生活方式对专注、记忆与理解能力的启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s26162.pcdn.co/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/AN-AMERICAN-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://s26162.pcdn.co/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/AN-AMERICAN-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48425611' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48425611' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mashable.com/tech/motorola-wifi-routers-stop-working-motosync-plus-app-down' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Motorola effectively bricked its entire line of WiFi routers without explanation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>摩托罗拉的整系列WiFi路由器被强制变砖，原因不明</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 07:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48425611'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Motorola reportedly pushed firmware changes that bricked an entire line of Wi-Fi routers without clear explanation, leaving users with nonfunctional devices. The incident underscores fragility of consumer networking hardware and the need for safer update practices and transparent rollback options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，摩托罗拉在固件更新后让整系列路由器变砖，原因未作明确解释，用户设备无法使用。这凸显了消费级网络硬件的脆弱性，以及需要更安全的更新策略与透明的回滚选项。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://helios-i.mashable.com/imagery/articles/04pNrBBhcbeOIgvgoXU72gn/hero-image.fill.size_1200x675.v1780680965.jpg' data-full='https://helios-i.mashable.com/imagery/articles/04pNrBBhcbeOIgvgoXU72gn/hero-image.fill.size_1200x675.v1780680965.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48403377' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48403377' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.boxofcables.dev/wsl2-per-device-swiotlb-pools-for-virtiofs-and-virtioproxy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WSL 2 is getting faster Windows file system access</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WSL 2 将提升对 Windows 文件系统的访问速度</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 12:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48403377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48403377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48403377'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WSL 2 gains faster Windows file system access thanks to per-device swiotlb pools and related optimizations for virtiofs and virtioproxy. The change reduces I/O bottlenecks for Linux workloads on Windows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WSL 2 将通过为每个设备提供 swiotlb 池等优化，提升对 Windows 文件系统的访问速度。这有助于在 Windows 上运行的 Linux 应用获得更好 I/O 性能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.boxofcables.dev/content/images/size/w1200/2026/05/Screenshot-2026-05-31-174734.png' data-full='https://www.boxofcables.dev/content/images/size/w1200/2026/05/Screenshot-2026-05-31-174734.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48311587' data-tags='Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48311587' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blainsmith.com/articles/httptrace-with-go/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tracing HTTP Requests with Go&#x27;s net/HTTP/httptrace</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Go 的 net/HTTP/httptrace 跟踪 HTTP 请求</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 28, 2026 / 09:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48311587' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 178</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48311587' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48311587'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Go&#x27;s httptrace package lets you drill into per-request timings across the HTTP stack, from DNS to TLS to connections. It can help diagnose bottlenecks and understand where latency originates in Go services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Go 的 httptrace 包允许你逐步跟踪 HTTP 请求在 DNS、连接、TLS 等阶段的耗时。它有助于定位瓶颈并理解性能问题在应用中的实际来源。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48370291'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 1993 overview of Fidonet, outlining its technology, typical tools, and user practices that powered early distributed bulletin-board networks. The document situates Fidonet in the pre-internet era as a grassroots, asynchronous communication backbone for hobbyists and regional networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份1993年的资料回顾了 Fidonet 的技术架构、常用工具与使用习惯，揭示其如何支撑早期分布式的电子公告网络。它将 Fidonet 放在互联网兴起前的社区型、异步通信网络背景中，强调业余者和地区网络的作用。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece vents about the enduring utility and quirks of rsync, using it as a lens on how longstanding open-source tools shape modern workflows. It confronts frustration with design decisions, maintenance, or open-source governance, and argues for clearer communication and sustainable stewardship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文围绕久经考验的 rsync 工具及其现代使用中的种种争议展开讨论，将其视作观察点来审视开源工具如何影响当前工作流程。文章表达对某些设计选择、维护方式或开源治理的挫折感，并倡议更清晰的沟通与可持续的治理。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48318624' data-tags='Science,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48318624' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cryptography101.ca/wp-content/uploads/lattice-based-cryptography.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Gentle Introduction to Lattice-Based Cryptography [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>格基密码学入门（PDF）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 28, 2026 / 20:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48318624' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48318624' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48318624'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This gentle introduction offers an accessible tour of lattice-based cryptography, outlining why hard lattice problems underpin quantum-resistant security. It covers core constructs like LWE and SIS in practical terms and discusses how these ideas translate into real-world cryptosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文以通俗方式介绍格基密码学的核心概念，解释为何格的困难问题构成量子安全性的基石。聚焦 LWE、SIS 等关键构造及其在实际密码系统中的应用要点，便于读者把握该领域的要点。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48409474' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48409474' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unix.stackexchange.com/questions/18154/what-is-the-purpose-of-the-lostfound-folder-in-linux-and-unix' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is the purpose of the lost+found folder in Linux and Unix? (2014)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux/Unix 的 lost+found 文件夹有什么用？（2014）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 01:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48409474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48409474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48409474'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The lost+found folder stores inodes recovered after filesystem checks, acting as a repository for orphaned fragments following a crash. It’s mostly of interest to those doing recovery or forensics, not day-to-day users. The feature embodies how Linux/Unix handles recovery while preserving data remnants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>lost+found 文件夹是在 Linux/Unix 文件系统自检修复（fsck）时用于存放恢复的 inode 的位置。它保存着在崩溃后被重建出的碎片和孤儿数据，通常并不面向普通用户。其存在的意义在于协助从损坏的文件系统中找回数据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://unix.stackexchange.com/Content/Sites/unix/Img/apple-touch-icon@2.png?v=32fb07f7ce26' data-full='https://unix.stackexchange.com/Content/Sites/unix/Img/apple-touch-icon@2.png?v=32fb07f7ce26' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48372138' data-tags='Show HN'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48372138' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://uonr.github.io/poincake/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Infinite canvas notes in the non-Euclidean Poincaré disk</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 09:08 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN presents an infinite canvas notes app built on the non-Euclidean Poincaré disk. It explores how hyperbolic geometry can influence information density and note-taking UX.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了一款在非欧几里得庞加莱圆盘上实现的无限画布笔记应用，探索双向缩放和信息密度在超曲率空间中的应用。通过将几何学引入用户界面，展示了新型笔记工具的潜力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discuss.python.org/t/an-announcement-from-the-steering-council-regarding-the-jit-project/107638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Python JIT project was asked to pause development</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Python JIT 项目被要求暂停开发</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 08:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48425982'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Python JIT project was asked to pause development, delaying work on just-in-time compilation. The move reflects governance and risk considerations within the Python ecosystem and could slow performance improvements in the near term.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Python JIT 项目被要求暂停开发，推迟即时编译相关工作。此举反映了 Python 生态内的治理与风险考量，短期内可能放慢性能改进的步伐。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/society/2026/may/31/daily-pill-daraxonrasib-double-survival-time-pancreatic-pancreas-cancer-clinical-trial' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Daily pill can double survival time for deadliest cancer, trial shows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每日药丸可使最致命的癌症生存期翻倍，临床试验显示</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 08:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48346629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 170</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48346629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48346629'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A daily pill shows potential to double survival time in pancreatic cancer in a clinical trial. If replicated, this could change treatment standards and spur further investment in targeted cancer therapies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>每日口服药物可使胰腺癌患者的生存期翻倍，临床试验显示。若进一步验证，这将改变治疗标准并推动针对性药物研发。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ableton released a software development kit enabling third-party developers to create extensions for Live, expanding the ecosystem beyond scripts and Max for Live. The move broadens opportunities for musicians and plugin developers to build deeper integrations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ableton 发布了可供第三方开发者为 Live 创建扩展的软件开发工具包，拓展了生态系统，超越了脚本和 Live 的 Max。此举为音乐人和插件开发者提供了更多深度集成的机会。</p>
