<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48435584' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jamanetwork.com/journals/jamanetworkopen/fullarticle/2849122' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vitamin D3 During Pregnancy and Cognitive Performance at 10 Years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>孕期维生素D3 与10岁认知表现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 08:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48435584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48435584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48435584'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A JAMA Network Open study finds associations between maternal Vitamin D3 supplementation during pregnancy and child cognitive performance at age 10, though causality is not established. It adds to evidence on prenatal nutrition&#x27;s long-term impact and may influence recommendations for prenatal care.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>JAMA Network Open 的研究表明，孕期补充维生素D3与孩子在10岁时的认知表现存在关联，但尚不能证明因果关系。该研究为产前营养对长期认知影响提供证据，或影响产前护理建议。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48419571' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48419571' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andersmurphy.com/2026/06/05/the-perils-of-uuid-primary-keys-in-sqlite.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The perils of UUID primary keys in SQLite</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SQLite 中 UUID 主键的隐患</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 16:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48419571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48419571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48419571'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article warns that using UUIDs as primary keys in SQLite can degrade performance due to random access and index fragmentation. It offers guidance to prefer INTEGER keys or optimized strategies to preserve query speed and storage efficiency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章警示在 SQLite 中将 UUID 作为主键可能因随机访问与索引碎片化导致性能下降，给出偏好使用 INTEGER 主键或其他优化策略以维持查询速度与存储效率的建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://andersmurphy.com/assets/avatar.png' data-full='https://andersmurphy.com/assets/avatar.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>度假时胰岛素泵发生故障</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 28, 2026 / 11:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48313427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48313427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48313427'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal account of insulin pump failure during vacation, revealing how medical IoT devices introduce new risks when away from home. It underscores the importance of backup plans, sensor redundancy, and support options for patients relying on connected devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>度假期间胰岛素泵发生故障，暴露了医用物联网设备在离家时的新风险。强调备份方案、传感器冗余和紧急支援对依赖连网设备的患者的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(206) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tokenomics: Quantifying Where Tokens Are Used in Agentic Software Engineering</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代币经济学：在具备代理能力的软件工程中令牌的使用位置量化</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 18:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48430923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48430923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48430923'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An arXiv preprint quantifies how tokens are used within agentic software systems, mapping economic incentives to agent behavior. The work helps researchers understand token flows, governance, and potential monetization in AI-driven engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇 arXiv 论文量化了在具备代理能力的软件系统中令牌的使用场景，绘制出令牌激励如何影响代理行为的映射。该研究为理解基于 AI 的工程中的代币流动、治理和潜在的商业化提供了框架。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48368725' data-tags='Science,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48368725' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apnews.com/article/notre-dame-dig-treasures-paris-archaeology-roman-dae41f792c1402faf32a87c154cc9a77' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Under Notre Dame, a &#x27;dig of the century&#x27; unearths 1,700 years of history</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>巴黎圣母院地下的‘世纪挖掘’揭露1700年的历史遗存</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 04:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48368725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48368725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48368725'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A major archaeology dig beneath Notre Dame in Paris has unearthed artifacts spanning 1,700 years, offering new insight into the city’s Roman and medieval past. The finds include everyday objects and remnants of ancient streets, reframing how historians view Paris’s urban development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>巴黎圣母院地下的重大考古挖掘揭露了跨越1700年的文物，为重新理解这座城市的罗马与中世纪历史提供新线索。出土的日常用品和古街遗迹等发现，重新勾勒了巴黎的城市演变过程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/64b0096/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4240x2825+0+3/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2F28%2Fff%2Ffc6a26514a1cd913e557c4e80e65%2F212b7fe79d074b8dbcc5ad791ed773fa' data-full='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/64b0096/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4240x2825+0+3/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2F28%2Fff%2Ffc6a26514a1cd913e557c4e80e65%2F212b7fe79d074b8dbcc5ad791ed773fa' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48349527' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48349527' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/health/2026/05/us-healthcare-still-stupidly-expensive-with-pathetic-outcomes-study-finds/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US healthcare still stupidly expensive, with pathetic outcomes, study finds</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new study confirms US healthcare remains exorbitantly expensive with subpar outcomes compared to peers. The findings highlight cost drivers, inefficiencies, and opportunities for tech-enabled care and policy reform.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究再次指出美国医疗成本高昂且结果堪忧，与国际同行相比效率偏低。研究呼吁通过科技驱动的护理创新和政策改革来改善现状。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ataraxy-labs.github.io/sem/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sem: New primitive for code understanding – not LSPs, but entities on top of Git</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Sem：用于代码理解的新原语——非 LSP，而是基于 Git 的实体</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 13:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48428475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 162</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48428475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48428475'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sem introduces a new primitive for code understanding that relies on entities anchored in Git rather than language server protocols. It aims to improve cross-repo code comprehension, navigation, and tooling, potentially enabling more scalable code intelligence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sem 提出用于代码理解的新原语，基于 Git 实体而非语言服务器协议。该方法拟提升跨仓库的代码理解、导航与工具集成，可能推动更可扩展的代码智能。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RubyGems adds a cooldown for new gems to undergo vetting before publication. The change aims to improve security and quality in the ecosystem, even if it slows new gem releases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RubyGems 为 Bundler 引入冷却期，对新 gem 进行发表前的审查与验证，提升生态系统的安全性与质量，尽管这可能放慢新 gem 的发布速度。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48375150' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openrepair.org/open-data/open-standard/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open Repair Data Standard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开放修复数据标准</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 12:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48375150' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48375150' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48375150'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open Repair Data Standard introduces a common schema to structure repair information—parts, procedures, warranties—so repair tooling and knowledge can interoperate. By standardizing repair data, it lowers barriers to DIY and commercial repair, reduces waste, and empowers consumers and makers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开放修复数据标准引入一个通用数据结构，用于整理维修信息中的部件、步骤与保修等，以实现修理工具和知识的互操作。标准化有助于降低 DIY 与商业修复的门槛，减少电子废弃物，提升消费者与创客的能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://openrepair.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/01/ords-data-collected.png' data-full='https://openrepair.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/01/ords-data-collected.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48379669' data-tags='Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48379669' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://research.ligo.bio/posts/unreasonable-redundancy-of-natural-protein-folds/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Unreasonable Redundancy of Nature&#x27;s Protein Folds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自然蛋白折叠的非理性冗余</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 20:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48379669' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48379669' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48379669'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how natural protein folds exhibit redundancy, with many sequences converging to a limited set of structures. Implications for protein design, evolution, and biotech; suggests that nature&#x27;s design space is more constrained than expected, guiding AI approaches to protein engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨自然蛋白折叠的冗余性：大量不同序列会聚于有限的结构类型。这对蛋白设计、进化生物学和生物技术具有意义，提示自然的设计空间比预期更受限，并为使用人工智能进行蛋白工程提供了指引。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://research.ligo.bio/assets/ligobio-logo-black.png' data-full='https://research.ligo.bio/assets/ligobio-logo-black.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(218) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://symbolica.io/posts/symbolica_2_0_release/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Symbolica 2.0: Programmable Symbols for Python and Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Symbolica 2.0：为 Python 与 Rust 提供的可编程符号</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 10:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48415457' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48415457' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48415457'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Symbolica 2.0 expands programmable symbolic computation to Python and Rust, enabling developers to manipulate symbolic expressions as first-class constructs. This opens avenues for symbolic math, theorem proving, and advanced code reasoning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Symbolica 2.0 将可编程符号计算扩展到 Python 与 Rust，使开发者能够将符号表达式作为一等公民来操作。这为符号化数学、定理证明和高级代码推理打开了新路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://symbolica.io/icon_center.svg' data-full='https://symbolica.io/icon_center.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48440064' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(219) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ranpara.net/posts/perceptron-explained-from-scratch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Smallest Brain You Can Build: A Perceptron in Python</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你能构建的最小大脑：用 Python 实现感知机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 17:28 PT</span></p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>From-scratch, it implements a single-layer perceptron in Python, covering weights, bias, and the basic learning rule. The write-up emphasizes intuition and practical code you can run to see how simple linear classifiers learn. A handy primer for getting hands-on with neural networks before moving to deeper models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>从零开始用 Python 实现一个简单的感知机，讲解权重、偏置和学习规则等核心概念。该文注重直观理解，给出可运行的代码，帮助你直观感受线性分类器的学习过程。是入门神经网络的实用入门材料。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://knowablemagazine.org/content/article/technology/2026/cloned-polo-horses' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Field of clones: How horse replicas came to dominate polo</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>克隆马场：克隆马如何主导马球运动</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 19:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48431286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48431286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48431286'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advances in animal cloning have flooded polo with high-fidelity replicas, changing breeding economics and the dynamics of the sport.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过基因克隆和现代育种技术，克隆马大量进入马球领域，改变了竞技马的供给与竞赛格局。文章讨论了克隆马在经济、伦理和比赛表现方面的影响，以及它们如何改变了这项运动的未来。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 08:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48426022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48426022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>England and Wales police were instructed to halt AI usage in court statements, highlighting concerns about the reliability and admissibility of AI-generated content in legal settings. The guidance signals regulatory caution as AI tools spread through law enforcement workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英格兰和威尔士警方被指示暂停在法庭陈述中使用 AI，凸显 AI 生成内容在法律环境中的可靠性与可采性问题。相关指引表明监管层对 AI 工具进入执法流程保持谨慎态度。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(225) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://idlewords.com/talks/superintelligence.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Superintelligence: The Idea That Eats Smart People (2016)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>超级智能：吞噬聪明人的想法（2016）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 10:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48360137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48360137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 214</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This 2016 talk critiques how the idea of superintelligence tends to attract top minds in tech and philosophy, while probing the real progress and risks of AI. It argues for grounded thinking about AI safety and alignment and cautions against overblown doomsday scenarios.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这场2016年的演讲批判了“超级智能”这一概念为何吸引大量聪明人，并探讨了人工智能的现实进展与潜在风险。演讲主张以务实的态度看待 AI 对齐与对未来情景的推演，警惕空想带来的偏差。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48362255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48362255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A bright meteor exploded over Massachusetts, shocking residents and generating numerous sightings. Scientists are still assessing the meteor&#x27;s trajectory and whether any fragments reached the ground, underscoring the usefulness of sky cameras and crowdsourced reports in tracking near-Earth objects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>马萨诸塞州上空出现一道浓艳的流星并发生爆炸，引发大量目击报道。科学家正在评估其轨迹与是否有碎片落地，这凸显了天文观测网络和大众参与数据在跟踪近地天体方面的作用。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48415987' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(227) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.flyingpenguin.com/cloudflare-ceo-is-lying-to-you-about-the-bot-traffic-jump/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare CEO is lying to you about the bot traffic jump</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare CEO 就机器人流量激增的问题在撒谎</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 10:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48415987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48415987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 136</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48415987'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical critique argues that Cloudflare&#x27;s public messaging about a bot traffic surge misrepresents the data, potentially conflating bots with legitimate traffic or using flawed measurement. The piece urges readers to scrutinize vendor claims and consider how bot metrics affect pricing, security posture, and trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇批评指出 Cloudflare 在关于机器人流量激增的说法中存在误导，可能将机器人流量与真实流量混淆，或采用有缺陷的统计方法。作者呼吁读者仔细审视厂商声明，并关注这类指标如何影响定价、安全策略与信任。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(228) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/develop-physical-ai-reasoning-world-and-action-models-with-nvidia-cosmos-3/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia Cosmos 3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英伟达 Cosmos 3</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 06:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48356654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48356654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48356654'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA Cosmos 3 is a platform for physical AI reasoning, enabling agents to model the world and actions with improved simulation. It advances robotics research by enabling integrated perception, planning, and control in realistic simulations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA Cosmos 3 是英伟达在物理AI推理领域的平台，提升了对世界与行动的建模与仿真能力。它让机器人研究者在真实感更强的仿真环境中更好地整合感知、规划与控制。</p>

</div>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48393252' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://biohub.org/news/world-model-of-protein-biology/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Biohub releases a world model of protein biology</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 20:12 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Biohub released a world model for protein biology, aggregating knowledge to simulate protein behavior at scale. The model could accelerate protein design and understanding, with implications for drug discovery and synthetic biology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Biohub 发布了一个蛋白质生物学的世界模型，汇聚知识以大规模模拟蛋白质行为。该模型有望加速蛋白质设计与理解，对药物发现和合成生物学具有深远意义。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 11:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48416635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48416635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48416635'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study argues that transformer architectures are inherently succinct, achieving strong performance with compact attention patterns. This challenges assumptions about model size and points to efficiency-informed directions for future model design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明，Transformer 架构本质上具备简洁性，通过紧凑的注意力模式实现出色表现。这挑战了关于模型规模的常规观念，指向未来在高效性方面的设计方向。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48405019' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48405019' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xeiaso.net/notes/2026/ipv6-zones-go-url/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IPv6 zones in URLs are a mistake</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>URL 中的 IPv6 区域标识符是错误的</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 14:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48405019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 144</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48405019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48405019'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>URL IPv6 zone identifiers lead to fragile links and inconsistent user experiences across browsers and copy-paste sharing. The author argues against embedding zone IDs in URLs, proposing alternatives like DNS-based routing or avoiding zones altogether to keep URLs portable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 URL 中使用 IPv6 区域标识符会让链接变脆、在浏览器与粘贴分享时表现不一致。作者反对在 URL 中嵌入区域标识，建议改用基于 DNS 的路由或避免区域标识来保持 URL 的可移植性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://xeiaso.net/.within.website/x/cmd/anubis/static/img/pensive.webp?cacheBuster=v1.25.1-0.20260604200537-44d5fa3ce047' data-full='https://xeiaso.net/.within.website/x/cmd/anubis/static/img/pensive.webp?cacheBuster=v1.25.1-0.20260604200537-44d5fa3ce047' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(235) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/huawei-csl/KVarN' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>KVarN: Native vLLM backend for KV-cache quantization by Huawei</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>KVarN：华为的本地 vLLM 后端实现 KV 缓存量化</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 08:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48399974' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 143</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48399974' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48399974'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>KVarN is a native backend for vLLM, focused on quantizing KV cache to speed up inference and reduce memory footprint. This work aims to improve large language model deployments in Huawei&#x27;s ecosystem by making caching more efficient.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>KVarN 是一个本地化的 vLLM 后端，针对 KV 缓存进行量化优化，提升推理速度并降低显存占用。该实现有助于在华为生态中更高效地部署大语言模型，提升缓存效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b65f4c1540f256b51cfa38bc2ea852f62b017f4289a7a25446f8d7697c7f5c5f/huawei-csl/KVarN' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b65f4c1540f256b51cfa38bc2ea852f62b017f4289a7a25446f8d7697c7f5c5f/huawei-csl/KVarN' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Groq continues to raise money, maintaining investor interest in its AI accelerator hardware despite a crowded market. The fundraising narrative highlights appetite for specialized AI chips, strategic partnerships, and the challenges of turning hype into revenue.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Groq 继续筹集资金，尽管市场竞争激烈，仍获得投资者关注，显示对AI 加速器硬件的需求。报道分析了对专业 AI 芯片的资金胃口、潜在的战略合作及将热度转化为营收的挑战，并讨论 Groq 如何定位自己以服务于各类 AI 工作负载。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(237) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://montanaskeptic.substack.com/p/the-spacex-ipo-will-be-the-theft' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The SpaceX IPO will be the theft of the century</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SpaceX 的 IPO 将是世纪盗窃</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 21:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48394034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48394034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48394034'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take arguing that SpaceX&#x27;s IPO would transfer vast wealth from the public to insiders, with concerns about mispricing, governance, and market impact. It warns about the broader implications for capital markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇对 SpaceX 首次公开募股的分析认为，IPO 将把巨额财富从公众转移给内部人，质疑定价、治理与对市场的潜在影响，并讨论对资本市场的更广泛后果。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48368291' data-tags='Web,Design,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48368291' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(238) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dan-webnotes.com/posts/2026-06-02-css-native-parallax-effect/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CSS-Native Parallax Effect</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CSS 原生视差效果</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 03:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48368291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48368291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48368291'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A CSS-native parallax effect achieves depth using only CSS properties—translate, perspective, and scroll-driven features—without JavaScript. The approach can deliver smoother, more accessible animations with fewer runtime costs, but requires careful cross-browser testing. The post likely showcases practical patterns and pitfalls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>原生 CSS 的视差效果仅靠 CSS 属性（如 transform、perspective 和滚动相关特性）实现纵向深度感，无需 JavaScript。此做法在减少运行成本的同时可能提升可访问性与流畅性，但需要仔细进行跨浏览器测试。文中给出实用的实现模式与要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dan-webnotes.com/assets/og-image.jpeg' data-full='https://dan-webnotes.com/assets/og-image.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48400151' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48400151' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(239) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fergusfinn.com/blog/kv-entropy-coder/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Speculative KV coding: losslessly compressing KV cache by up to ~4×</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>推测性 KV 编码：无损压缩 KV 缓存，提升约 4×</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 08:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48400151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48400151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48400151'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Speculative KV coding introduces a lossless compression technique for key-value caches, achieving up to about 4x memory savings. By predicting likely keys/values and applying entropy coding, the technique reduces memory pressure and improves cache efficiency in high-throughput systems. The approach is domain-specific but demonstrates how predictive coding can unlock meaningful gains without sacrificing data integrity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出一种对 KV 缓存进行无损压缩的推测性编码，理论上内存占用可降低至原来的约 4 倍。通过对可能的键值进行预测并使用熵编码实现压缩，该方法在高吞吐缓存场景下有望降低内存压力、提升缓存命中率，同时保持数据完整性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fergusfinn.com/saral-og.jpg' data-full='https://fergusfinn.com/saral-og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 02:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48409955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48409955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48409955'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lowfat is a pluggable CLI filter that dramatically reduces LLM token usage by trimming input before prompting. It helps cut costs and speed up interactions in LLM workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lowfat 是一个可插拔的 CLI 过滤器，通过在提示前对输入进行裁剪，显著降低 LLM 令牌消耗，降低成本并提升响应速度。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48369864' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48369864' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(248) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.autodidacts.io/social-cache-busting/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Social Cache Busting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>社交缓存失效策略</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 06:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48369864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48369864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48369864'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Social caches on networks often serve stale previews long after you push updates. The post outlines practical cache-busting techniques for social assets, including versioned URLs, query-string parameters, and targeted CDN invalidations, plus aligning Open Graph data to ensure fresh previews. For developers shipping content updates, proactive cache strategy matters for engagement and trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>社交平台对内容预览和资源有强缓存，更新后可能长时间显示旧信息。文章给出实际的缓存失效方法，如版本化资源、更改查询参数，以及针对 CDN 的定向失效策略，并强调与 Open Graph 数据对齐以确保新预览生效。对于持续更新的内容，这种缓存策略直接影响用户互动与信任感。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.autodidacts.io/content/images/2026/02/Hugo_Kauffmann_Im_Gespr--ch-small.jpg' data-full='https://www.autodidacts.io/content/images/2026/02/Hugo_Kauffmann_Im_Gespr--ch-small.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48440463' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48440463' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(249) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/new-drug-functionally-cures-many-hepatitis-b-virus-infections?user_id=66c4bf745d78644b3aa57b08' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New drug &#x27;functionally cures&#x27; many hepatitis B virus infections</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新药能在多数乙肝感染者身上实现功能性治愈</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 18:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48440463'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study reports a drug that functionally cures many hepatitis B infections, achieving sustained viral control after treatment in a substantial portion of patients. Functionally cured patients can discontinue therapy while maintaining low viral activity, representing a potential paradigm shift in HBV treatment. Still, larger trials and long-term follow-up are needed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项研究报道一种新药能在相当数量的乙型肝炎病毒感染者身上实现功能性治愈，治疗后可维持长期病毒控制。功能性治愈意味着在停止治疗后仍能保持低水平的病毒活性，可能改变乙肝治疗的格局。然而，仍需在更大规模的试验和长期随访中验证。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48404063' data-tags='Energy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48404063' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(250) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/graphics/2026-energy-transition-iran-war/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iran Shock Jolts Asia and Europe to Speed Up Energy Transition</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊朗冲击促使亚洲和欧洲加速能源转型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 13:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48404063' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48404063' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 113</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48404063'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Geopolitical shocks from Iran are nudging Asia and Europe to accelerate their energy transition plans. The graphics illustrate shifts in energy mixes, investments in renewables and LNG, and policy moves aimed at reducing reliance on volatile fossil fuels.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伊朗的地缘政治冲击推动亚洲与欧洲加速能源转型。相关图表展示能源结构的调整、对可再生能源与 LNG 的投资增加，以及降低对易变化石燃料依赖的政策举措。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48379664' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48379664' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(251) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.utoronto.ca/news/u-t-researchers-demonstrate-ai-worm-could-target-any-online-device' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U of T researchers demonstrate AI worm could target any online device</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>U of T researchers demonstrate AI worm could target any online device</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 20:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48379664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48379664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48379664'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate an AI-powered worm that could adaptively target online devices, illustrating how autonomous malware might evolve to map networks and exploit vulnerabilities. The result underscores the need for robust device hardening and proactive security updates. It’s a wake-up call for securing IoT and networked endpoints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员展示了一种 AI 驱动的蠕虫，能够自适应地定位并攻击在线设备，演示了自治型恶意软件如何在网络中自我进化。该成果凸显对设备加固和主动安全更新的迫切需求。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kiki is a tiny, lightweight homepage construction kit with a small footprint, designed for rapid assembly of simple personal sites. It emphasizes minimal dependencies and fast load times, ideal for static sites or resource-constrained environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kiki 是一个极简、体积小的首页构建工具，依赖极少，目标是快速搭建简单的个人主页。它强调低依赖和快速加载，适合静态站点或资源受限的场景。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48365904' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.janestreet.com/strace-ui-bonsai-term-and-the-tui-renaissance/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Strace-ui, Bonsai_term, and the TUI renaissance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Strace-ui、Bonsai_term 与 TUI 复兴</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 21:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48365904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 127</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48365904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48365904'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Strace-ui offers a graphical interface for tracing system calls, while Bonsai_term provides terminal UI tooling; together they exemplify a broader resurgence of terminal user interfaces. The trend reflects developers embracing richer, composable TUIs that blend usability with low-level accessibility, often powered by modern Rust or Go ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Strace-ui 为跟踪系统调用提供图形化界面，Bonsai_term 提供终端 UI 工具，这两者体现了 TUI（终端用户界面）复兴的趋势。开发者开始倾向于更丰富、可组合的终端界面，兼具易用性与对底层的可访问性，常见于现代的 Rust、Go 等生态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.janestreet.com/strace-ui-bonsai-term-and-the-tui-renaissance/bonsai-trees.gif' data-full='https://blog.janestreet.com/strace-ui-bonsai-term-and-the-tui-renaissance/bonsai-trees.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/1995966.1996008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nabokov&#x27;s pale fire: the lost &#x27;father of all hypertext demos&#x27;? (2011)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nabokov 的《盎然之火》：2011 年失落的“所有超文本演示之父”？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 18:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48351579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48351579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of Nabokov&#x27;s Pale Fire and its status as a potential early hypertext demonstration, offering a historical lens on the evolution of nonlinear writing and digital literature. The piece contextualizes how early experiments foreshadow modern hypertext and interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨《盎然之火》在超文本历史中的地位，作为早期超文本演示的候选之一。通过历史视角理解非线性叙事与数字文学的演变。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://isupmap.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The IsUpMap lets you check the status of over 100 major sites at once</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IsUpMap：一次性查看逾百大站点状态</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 21:44 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48408067'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IsUpMap aggregates uptime data for 100+ major sites, giving operators a quick pulse on global service health. It enables cross-site monitoring, API access, and faster incident response for IT and web teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>IsUpMap 将百余个大型站点的正常运行时间数据汇总，帮助运维人员快速把握全球服务健康状态。提供跨站点监控、API 访问，并提升 IT 与网页团队的应急响应速度。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(267) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mitxela.com/projects/wifi_time' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WiFi Time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WiFi Time：基于 WiFi 信标的时间源</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 07:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48370775' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48370775' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48370775'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WiFi Time demonstrates a compact device that derives accurate time from WiFi beacon timing, offering a low-cost, offline time source. It shows how commodity radio signals can be repurposed for reliable local timekeeping.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WiFi Time 展示一个使用 WiFi 信标时序来获取时间的紧凑设备，成本低且可离线使用，展示了将常见射频信号用于本地时间的可行性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48405815' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(268) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cen.acs.org/materials/nanomaterials/buckyballs-boron-buckminster-fullerene-nanomaterials/104/web/2026/06' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Introducing Boron Buckyballs: Theory that B80 cages can’t be made is disproved</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>硼原子球：B80 笼体理论被证伪</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 15:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48405815' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48405815' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48405815'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New experiments show B80 boron buckyballs can be synthesized, overturning prior belief that such cages were impossible. This advances nanomaterials and could impact materials design at the nanoscale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新实验表明可以合成 B80 硼簇笼体，推翻了此前“此类笼体无法制造”的理论。这推动了纳米材料领域的发展，或对纳米尺度的材料设计产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s7d1.scene7.com/is/content/CENODS/485651-bbb?ext=.gif' data-full='https://s7d1.scene7.com/is/content/CENODS/485651-bbb?ext=.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(269) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.hellas.ai/blog/thunderbolt-ibverbs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>thunderbolt-ibverbs: We have InfiniBand at home</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Thunderbolt-ibverbs：家用环境也能实现 InfiniBand</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 07:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48371008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48371008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48371008'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project demonstrates running InfiniBand verbs over Thunderbolt, bringing high-performance networking to consumer machines. By masking the complex hardware behind a familiar port, it enables hobbyists and researchers to experiment with RDMA-style communication without enterprise NICs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个项目展示了通过 Thunderbolt 将 InfiniBand 的 verbs 运作在家用设备上，带来高性能、低延迟的网络连接。该方案让爱好者和研究者在没有企业级网卡的情况下，也能在家里试验 RDMA 风格的通信。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(270) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gleam.run/news/single-file-gleam-beam-programs-with-escript/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gleam v1.17.0</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 15:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48377080' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48377080' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48377080'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gleam 1.17.0 ships as a single-file distribution that lets you run BEAM code with escript, simplifying packaging and execution of small Erlang/Elixir-like programs. The update emphasizes portability and quick experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gleam 1.17.0 作为单文件发行，允许通过 escript 运行 BEAM 代码，简化小型 BEAM 应用的打包与执行，强调可移植性和快速试验。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48402116' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(271) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/faster-dna-synthesis-sidewinder' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Leap in DNA synthesis slashes time to build new genetic sequences</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DNA 合成时间大幅缩短的跃进</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 10:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48402116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48402116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48402116'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A breakthrough in DNA synthesis slashes the time to build new genetic sequences, accelerating biotech design-build-test cycles. The advance likely stems from chemistry, automation, and scalability improvements with wide implications for research and rapid-response biology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DNA 合成在设计-构建-测试周期方面取得突破性缩短，加速新基因序列的构建。此进展可能源自化学方法、自动化与规模化提升，对研究效率和快速应对生物挑战具有广泛影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS releases Speech Services version 2, likely bringing on-device voice processing improvements, privacy enhancements, and better integration with the OS. <p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Podman 6 delivers machine usability improvements, simplifying how developers interact with machine-based workflows in the container ecosystem. The update tightens defaults, improves CLI ergonomics, and strengthens rootless operations. It lowers the barrier to productive, daemonless container workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Podman 6 对机器相关命令带来可用性改进，简化了上手与生命周期管理，降低非 Root 模式下使用容器的门槛。更新聚焦于提高开发者在日常容器工作流中的效率与自动化能力。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Databow is a Rust-based CLI that queries any database via an ADBC driver, enabling a unified workflow across backends. It signals growing interest in cross-database tooling and showcases Rust&#x27;s suitability for fast, safe data tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Databow 是一个基于 Rust 的命令行工具，通过 ADBC 驱动实现对任意数据库的查询，提供跨后端的统一工作流。体现了跨数据库工具的兴起，以及 Rust 在构建高性能、安全数据工具方面的适用性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(281) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/viggy28/streambed' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Streambed – Stream Postgres to Iceberg on S3, Supports Postgres Wire</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Streambed — 将 Postgres 数据流式传输到 Iceberg（S3），并支持 Postgres Wire</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 11:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48348429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48348429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48348429'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Streambed is an open-source tool that streams PostgreSQL data into Iceberg on S3, including support for the Postgres wire protocol. It simplifies real-time analytics architectures by enabling seamless data movement from transactional databases to a data lake format.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Streambed 是一款开源工具，可将 PostgreSQL 数据流式写入 S3 上的 Iceberg，并支持 Postgres wire 协议。它通过实现从事务数据库到数据湖格式的数据无缝移动，简化了实时分析架构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/12ced05c4108d6ff075a265cc91a85e03da69342b0fbf5f8e48e2b4c51569a63/viggy28/streambed' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/12ced05c4108d6ff075a265cc91a85e03da69342b0fbf5f8e48e2b4c51569a63/viggy28/streambed' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48377140' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(282) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://archaeologymag.com/2026/05/mohenjo-daro-grew-more-equal-over-time/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>4K years ago, Mohenjo-daro grew more equal over time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>莫亨佐-达城在漫长历史中逐渐趋向平等</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 15:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48377140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48377140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48377140'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archaeology suggests Mohenjo-daro grew more egalitarian over time, challenging notions of rigid ancient hierarchies. The study uses material culture and housing data to illustrate social dynamics in an urban center.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>考古学新证据表明莫亨佐-达城在漫长历史中逐渐趋向平等，挑战了对古代等级社会的刻板印象。研究基于居住分布与文物证据等，揭示了城市社会的动力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://archaeologymag.com/wp-content/uploads/mohenjo-daro-grew-more-equal-1.jpg' data-full='https://archaeologymag.com/wp-content/uploads/mohenjo-daro-grew-more-equal-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(283) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.capstone-engine.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Capstone – multi-platform, multi-architecture disassembly framework</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Capstone：跨平台、跨体系结构的反汇编框架</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 18:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48378925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48378925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48378925'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Capstone is a multi-platform, multi-architecture disassembly framework used by researchers and security engineers. Its cross-architecture support simplifies binary analysis pipelines and enables tooling across heterogeneous targets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Capstone 是一个跨平台、支持多架构的反汇编框架，被广泛用于逆向工程和二进制分析。新版持续扩展对不同处理器与平台的支持，提升分析流程的稳定性与灵活性。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48440792' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48440792' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(285) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://igerman.cc/blog/dopamine-fracking/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dopamine Fracking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多巴胺开采</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 19:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440792' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440792' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48440792'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of how apps weaponize dopamine triggers to lock in user attention, i.e., dopamine fracking. It discusses engineering tricks used to maximize engagement and the long-term costs to well-being. It suggests alternative design practices that respect users’ time and autonomy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批判性地分析应用如何利用多巴胺反馈机制来锁定用户注意力，即所谓的‘多巴胺开采’。文中揭示了被用来最大化黏性的一些工程手段及其对长期健康的成本，并提出更具道德性的产品设计思路，强调尊重用户时间与自主权。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://igerman.cc/processed_images/Synthetic_Krabby_Patty.313451d7779d4811.jpg' data-full='https://igerman.cc/processed_images/Synthetic_Krabby_Patty.313451d7779d4811.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(286) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/Firefox-Vulkan-Video-Merged' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firefox Merges Support for Vulkan Video Decoding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Firefox 将对 Vulkan 视频解码的支持合并到主线</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 15:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48439348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48439348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48439348'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox merges Vulkan-based video decoding support, enabling video playback to leverage modern GPU pipelines and potentially lower CPU usage, especially on Linux and Windows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 将对 Vulkan 视频解码的支持合并到主线，利用现代 GPU 流程实现更高效的视频解码，尤其在 Linux/Windows 上可降低 CPU 负载。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48410427' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48410427' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(289) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alvaro-videla.com/llm-arithmetic-internals/article_interactive/article.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Arithmetic Without Numbers – How LLMs Do Math</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>没有数字的算术——LLMs 如何做数学</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 03:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48410427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48410427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48410427'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LLMs don&#x27;t rely on explicit arithmetic; they compute by learned patterns and internal representations, sometimes supplemented by tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LLMs 做数学并非直接执行精确算术，而是通过学得的模式、内部表征以及有时借助外部工具来推理。文章解析了模型如何在没有显式数字执行下进行推算，以及提升数值准确性的潜在方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://alvaro-videla.com/llm-arithmetic-internals/article_interactive/assets/repo-card.png' data-full='https://alvaro-videla.com/llm-arithmetic-internals/article_interactive/assets/repo-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48335022' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48335022' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(290) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://categoricaldata.net/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CQL: Categorical Databases</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CQL：分类数据库</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 30, 2026 / 04:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48335022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48335022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48335022'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CQL proposes a categorical database model and accompanying query language that emphasizes labeled categories and typed relations, offering a different approach to modeling semi-structured data. The approach promises clearer semantics for domain-specific datasets and could influence how data teams model and query semi-structured information.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CQL 提出一种分类数据库模型及相应的查询语言，强调带标签的类别与类型化关系，以简化对结构化数据的分析。该设计有望为领域数据集带来更清晰的语义，并可能影响团队对半结构化信息的建模和查询方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://categoricaldata.net/logo.png' data-full='https://categoricaldata.net/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全信封模式集合——S.E.C.R.E.T 是一组经筛选的安全设计模式，旨在更好地界定并保护数据边界。它提供降低数据暴露、强化边界约束、完善防御深度的实用模式和工程示例。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48425347' data-tags='Security,Programming,Web'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在沙箱中运行 Python 代码：MicroPython 与 WASM</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 07:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48425347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48425347'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Running Python code in a sandbox with MicroPython and WASM offers a portable, secure sandbox for executing Python in constrained environments. This combination can enable embedded tooling or browser-based experiments with safer execution guarantees.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在沙箱中运行 Python 代码，使用 MicroPython 与 WASM 提供了一种便携、隔离的执行环境，便于在嵌入式设备或浏览器中安全地测试和运行 Python。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 11:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48402744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48402744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48402744'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that deferring actions with a &#x27;Maybe later&#x27; option can improve engagement by reducing friction, though some critique says it enables procrastination. It reframes a common UX pattern as a deliberate design choice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>认为“稍后再说”作为一种延迟行为的设计，可能通过降低使用摩擦来提高参与度，但也有观点认为它助长拖延。这把常见的 UX 模式当作有意的设计选择来解读。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48390688' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(303) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opensource.googleblog.com/2026/06/journey-to-jpeg-xl-how-open-source-experiments-shaped-the-future-of-image-coding.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Journey to JPEG XL: open-source experiments shaped the future of image coding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通往 JPEG XL 的旅程：开源实验塑造了图像编码的未来</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 14:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48390688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48390688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48390688'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JPEG XL emerged from a decade of open-source experimentation, aimed at delivering high visual quality at smaller file sizes with broad toolchain support. The story shows how community-driven testing helped shape a practical, royalty-free image format that competes with older standards, and how openness accelerated adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>JPEG XL 源自多年的开源实验，目标是在保持高画质的同时实现更小的文件尺寸，并获得广泛的工具链支持。开源社区的持续试验促成了一个实用、免版税的图像格式，与传统标准相比具备更强的落地能力与采用潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEic_WoSwZ84GajY2-xiBPt4P5ho2eN8-lyfLAx_8A_VfQpy-UKyJSxOjFDn118y4LCXy5EShCZ2L5NH2a5EC_cEh2WyY1oV6ZhipsJ2ZtYl7G2VRkGtP1YWU7m0Gq98NqEUCkcve9f4iDALjZ4diU1SFKXq60P_ba2FA1TP07Ovy8NLXrvVbWNCp1sueSE/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/jxl-graph.jpg' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEic_WoSwZ84GajY2-xiBPt4P5ho2eN8-lyfLAx_8A_VfQpy-UKyJSxOjFDn118y4LCXy5EShCZ2L5NH2a5EC_cEh2WyY1oV6ZhipsJ2ZtYl7G2VRkGtP1YWU7m0Gq98NqEUCkcve9f4iDALjZ4diU1SFKXq60P_ba2FA1TP07Ovy8NLXrvVbWNCp1sueSE/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/jxl-graph.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48343901'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The DOS ecosystem shows partial multicore support, with experiments around emulation and OS-level multithreading. Realistic multithreading on classic DOS remains limited but shows progress for enthusiasts and retrocomputing projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DOS 生态圈在多核方面取得部分实现，涉及仿真和操作系统级多线程的实验。尽管在经典 DOS 上实现真正的多线程仍有限，但对爱好者和边缘计算项目有一定进展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.vogons.org/avatars/36725_1508515720.gif' data-full='https://www.vogons.org/avatars/36725_1508515720.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48353504' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48353504' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(306) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gist.github.com/skipcloud/f1033afb4fa5681d69fa63458cc95928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using Git&#x27;s rerere feature to escape recurring conflict hell</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用 Git 的 rerere 功能摆脱反复冲突的地狱</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 00:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48353504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48353504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48353504'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Git rerere automates reusing resolved conflict hunks, cutting repetitive manual merges. The guide shows how to enable and leverage it for smoother collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Git 的 rerere 功能可以在多次合并冲突后复用已解决的代码片段，减少重复工作。文章演示如何启用并在协作中充分利用它来提升工作流的顺畅度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' data-full='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48410602' data-tags='Open Source,Politics,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48410602' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(307) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/library/communication-european-tech-sovereignty-accompanied-eu-open-source-strategy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Communication on European Tech Sovereignty, and an EU Open-Source Strategy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于欧洲科技主权的沟通与欧盟开源策略</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 03:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48410602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48410602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48410602'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU outlines a tech sovereignty agenda paired with an open-source strategy to bolster autonomy, cybersecurity, and digital infrastructure, with implications for developers, procurement, and cross-border cooperation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟发布科技主权相关的沟通并搭配开源策略，着力提升自治能力、网络安全和数字基础设施，对开发者、采购与跨境合作产生影响。</p>
