</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48347961' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48347961' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(308) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/104185/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Re: [PATCH] OOM_pardon, a.k.a. don&#x27;t kill my xlock (2004)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于 OOM_pardon：请别把我的 xlock 杀死</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 10:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48347961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48347961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48347961'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A patch discussion centers on protecting a specific process (xlock) from the Linux OOM killer, highlighting the tension between memory pressure and critical user-facing tasks. The change could improve stability for screensavers or lock screens but may shift memory pressure to other processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇补丁讨论聚焦于在内存不足时保护特定进程（xlock）不被 OOM killer 终止，凸显内存压力和关键用户任务之间的矛盾。该改动可能提升锁屏/屏幕保护程序的稳定性，但也可能把内存压力挪给其他进程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48324288' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48324288' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://groups.google.com/g/comp.unix.wizards/c/QX_dxElrVNs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Unix in East Germany (GDR) (1990)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1990 年的东德 Unix</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 29, 2026 / 08:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48324288' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48324288' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48324288'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look back at Unix adoption in East Germany around 1990, illustrating how scarcity, political context, and cross-border tech exchange shaped early computing culture in East Germany.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾1990年前后东德地区对 Unix 的采用，展示了资源短缺、政治环境和跨境技术交流如何共同塑造了东德早期的计算机文化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fonts.gstatic.com/s/i/productlogos/groups/v9/web-48dp/logo_groups_color_1x_web_48dp.png' data-full='https://fonts.gstatic.com/s/i/productlogos/groups/v9/web-48dp/logo_groups_color_1x_web_48dp.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48436819' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48436819' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(310) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.righto.com/2026/06/ibm-604-thyraton-tube-module.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Powering up a module from the IBM 604: an electronic calculator from 1948</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为 IBM 604 的模块上电：1948 年电子计算器的复原之旅</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 10:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48436819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48436819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48436819'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on look at powering up a modular unit from the IBM 604, a 1948 vacuum-tube calculator. It covers the challenges of reviving vintage hardware, including high-voltage power supplies and precise on/off sequencing. The project offers a tangible window into the engineering trade-offs that paved the way for later transistorized machines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章实地讲解如何为 IBM 604 的一个模块上电，展示了1948年真空管计算器的复原难点。内容聚焦高压供电、上电顺序等技术挑战，以及这类早期硬件在设计取舍上的历史教训。通过复原，可以直观感受推动后续晶体管化的工程演进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/blogger_img_proxy/AEn0k_s-NkbGPu7trHfdYh_gu7RvAz0_q1BuYfAxj2yqQopSBnd-pumfnVjtLnB6_zE0hNKtvIBmvxm75X9N5vFxL4UZr3dzJPL8Jg9CCyp3Il1F8fK_o3LqTKDRB3mCYadmcaZhoagZ1Hk=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/blogger_img_proxy/AEn0k_s-NkbGPu7trHfdYh_gu7RvAz0_q1BuYfAxj2yqQopSBnd-pumfnVjtLnB6_zE0hNKtvIBmvxm75X9N5vFxL4UZr3dzJPL8Jg9CCyp3Il1F8fK_o3LqTKDRB3mCYadmcaZhoagZ1Hk=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An offshore earthquake of magnitude 7.8 rattled southern Philippines, with a tsunami alert possibly issued; authorities urged evacuations and monitoring for aftershocks.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(312) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://drewdevault.com/blog/Circus-freaks-of-FOSS/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The circus freaks of open source</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源圈的马戏团怪胎</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 21:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48431952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48431952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48431952'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Drew DeVault critiques the loud, disruptive personalities and internal politics shaping open-source culture. The piece questions how drama and charisma influence project governance, funding, and collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德沃尔特批评了开源社区中喧嚣、具争议性的人物与内部政治对开源治理、资助与协作的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48404905' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48404905' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(313) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cen.acs.org/materials/biobased-materials/queen-bees-special-wax/104/web/2026/06' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Queen bees emerge from special wax chambers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蜂后从特殊蜡腔中孵化</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 14:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48404905' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48404905' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48404905'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers report that queen bees emerge from specially crafted wax chambers, shedding light on queen-rearing methods and materials science of bee wax. The finding has implications for beekeeping efficiency and potentially bio-inspired materials design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员在蜡质腔体中培育蜂后，观察蜂后从专门设计的蜡腔中孵化。该发现为养蜂实践提供新方法，同时也为蜡质材料的生物学研究和仿生材料设计提供灵感。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s7d1.scene7.com/is/image/CENODS/How-worker-bees-use-wax-chemistry-to-sculpt-a-future-queen---488257:Widescreen?$responsive$&amp;qlt=90,0&amp;resMode=sharp2&amp;fmt=webp' data-full='https://s7d1.scene7.com/is/image/CENODS/How-worker-bees-use-wax-chemistry-to-sculpt-a-future-queen---488257:Widescreen?$responsive$&amp;qlt=90,0&amp;resMode=sharp2&amp;fmt=webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48414479' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48414479' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(314) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/the-u-s-military-quietly-turned-gps-into-a-global-numbers-station-evidence-suggests/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. Military Turned GPS into a Global &quot;Numbers Station&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国军方将 GPS 变成全球‘数字号码台’</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 09:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48414479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48414479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48414479'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report argues the U.S. military turned GPS into a global numbers station, effectively turning satellites into a covert broadcast channel. The piece underscores how GPS-dependent infrastructure can be leveraged for hidden communications and the security implications of signal manipulation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称美军将 GPS 转变为全球数字号码台，实质上使卫星成为隐蔽广播通道。这揭示了以 GPS 依赖的基础设施可能被用于隐藏通信，以及信号操控带来的安全隐忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/image1.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/0f/76/0f76b548-bc58-4f25-abc3-3f5ebca07da4/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/image1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48370558' data-tags='Design,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48370558' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(315) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.designboom.com/design/3d-printed-book-manual-darius-ou-benson-chong/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>3D-printed book turns its own G-code into raised lettering</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>3D 打印书籍将自身 G-code 变成浮雕字</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 07:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48370558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 82</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48370558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48370558'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 3D-printed book uses its own G-code to generate raised lettering, creating a tactile typographic effect. The project showcases self-referential fabrication and demonstrates how digital instructions can directly manifest as accessible, embossed text, blending design, 3D printing, and printed media.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一本3D打印的书将自身的G-code转化为浮雕字，实质在印刷品上呈现凸起的文本。这一自指式设计展示了数字指令到物理排版的直接转化，融合设计、3D 打印与可访问性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.designboom.com/twitterimages/uploads/2026/05/darius-ou-manual-3D-printed-book-designboom-FB.jpg' data-full='https://www.designboom.com/twitterimages/uploads/2026/05/darius-ou-manual-3D-printed-book-designboom-FB.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48316350' data-tags='Science,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48316350' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(316) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thereader.mitpress.mit.edu/the-genius-of-the-barn-owls-feathers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Genius of the Barn Owl&#x27;s Feathers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>猫头鹰羽毛的巧妙之处</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 28, 2026 / 15:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48316350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48316350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48316350'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Barn owl feathers conceal microstructures that cancel noise as air flows through and around the wing, enabling near-silent flight. The piece links this natural ingenuity to biomimetic research aimed at quieter drones, wind turbines, and acoustic materials.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>猫头鹰羽毛的微观结构能抑制空气中的涡流和噪音，从而实现接近无声的飞行。文章将这一自然智慧与仿生研究联系起来，展示在无人机、风力发电机和声学材料领域的应用潜力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Opus 4.8 introduces formally verified polygon intersection, marking a milestone after previous failures. The project demonstrates practical formal verification tooling for computational geometry and improves confidence in geometric algorithms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Opus 4.8 提供了多边形相交问题的形式化验证，通过一次性通过的成就克服了此前的失败。该工作展示了在计算几何领域应用形式化工具的可行性和信心提升。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(322) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.learnix-os.com/ch02-03-implementing-the-bitfields-proc-macro.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building Rust Procedural Macros from the Grounds Up</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从零开始构建 Rust 的过程宏</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 12:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48348876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48348876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48348876'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Procedural macros are a powerful Rust feature for meta-programming, letting you generate code at compile time. Building them from scratch introduces patterns for parsing token streams, hygiene, and error reporting, and shows how to implement domain-specific macros like bitfield helpers. This practical guide helps Rust developers unlock deeper code generation capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>过程宏是 Rust 的强大元编程特性，能在编译时生成代码。从头实现它们能学习解析标记流、词法作用域等设计模式，并演示如何实现领域特定的宏，如位域助手。这份实用指南有助于开发者掌握更深层的代码生成能力。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48387095' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(323) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48387095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Hyper (YC P26) – Company brain to power agentic development</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Launch HN：Hyper（YC P26）——推动代理式开发的公司大脑</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 10:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48387095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48387095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48387095'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hyper, a YC P26 alumnus, positions itself as a &#x27;company brain&#x27; to power agentic development—orchestrating AI agents across an organization. The platform aims to turn corporate data, workflows, and governance into actionable autonomous capabilities, reducing manual toil for developers and operators. It signals a shift toward toolchains that manage AI agents at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hyper（YC P26）定位为“公司大脑”，用于驱动代理式开发并在组织层面编排 AI 代理。该平台将企业数据、工作流与治理转化为可执行的自主能力，帮助开发者与运维人员减轻重复性工作。预示着面向大规模 AI 代理的工具链正在成形。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48437568' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48437568' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(324) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31514' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If LLMs Have Human-Like Attributes, Then So Does Age of Empires II</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>若 LLM 拥有类人特征，那么《帝国时代 II》也具备</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 11:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437568' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437568' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48437568'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper argues that AoE II&#x27;s strategic, long-horizon planning mimics certain human-like reasoning patterns, offering a testbed for evaluating LLM-like attributes. It cautions against over-mapping human traits to AI and highlights the value of domain-specific benchmarks in AI evaluation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文把《帝国时代 II》中的长期规划，与大语言模型的类人特征联系起来，作为评估人类式推理特征的一个测试基准。研究提醒在 AI 评估中不要过度拟人化，并强调领域特定基准的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(325) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.alexself.dev/blog/automated-doubt' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My automated doubt development process</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 11:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437305' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437305' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48437305'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>My automated doubt development process describes a workflow that surfaces or generates doubts during reasoning to curb bias and improve decisions. It outlines how automation can replay scenarios, challenge assumptions, and log doubts for later review. A meta-tool for more disciplined thinking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者描述了一种自动化过程，用于在推理中产出或揭示怀疑点，以抵消偏见并提升决策质量。文中可能讲解如何通过自动化重现情景、挑战假设，并将疑问记录以便复盘。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48360414' data-tags='DevOps,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48360414' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(326) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sigwait.org/~alex/blog/2026/05/28/smdBC8.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making Debian or Fedora persistent live images</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>制作 Debian 或 Fedora 的持久化实时镜像</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 11:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48360414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48360414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48360414'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes methods to create persistent live USB images for Debian or Fedora, enabling the OS to remember changes across reboots. Useful for field work, demonstrations, and offline environments where a portable OS must retain updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍如何制作 Debian 或 Fedora 的持久化实时镜像，使系统在重启后保留修改。适用于野外工作、演示场景或需要离线环境的便携式操作系统。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48343435' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(327) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.washi.dev/posts/custom-attributes-and-why-they-suck/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Custom Attributes in .NET Give Me Nightmares</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>.NET 自定义属性为何让我头疼</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 30, 2026 / 23:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48343435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48343435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48343435'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues custom attributes in .NET can be brittle, opaque, and lead to maintenance headaches due to reflection, versioning, and metadata pitfalls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中指出 .NET 的自定义属性可能带来脆弱性、不可直观理解以及反射、版本兼容等元数据问题，给维护带来隐患。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.washi.dev/assets/img/avatar.transparent.png' data-full='https://blog.washi.dev/assets/img/avatar.transparent.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48384525' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48384525' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(328) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.ydb.tech/do-we-fear-the-serializable-isolation-level-more-than-we-fear-subtle-bugs-5a025401b609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Do we fear the serializable isolation level more than we fear subtle bugs (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们是否更担心可串行化隔离级别，而非微妙的错误？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 07:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48384525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48384525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48384525'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Serializable isolation promises correctness but often adds complexity and performance costs that may cause more pain than the rare, subtle bugs it aims to prevent. The piece advocates pragmatic cross-checks, selective isolation, and robust testing to balance safety with practicality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>可串行化隔离级别承诺正确性，但往往带来额外的复杂性与性能成本，反而让少数罕见的微妙错误带来更大痛点。文中倡导务实的并发检测、选择性隔离与充分测试，以实现安全性与实际可行性的平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://miro.medium.com/v2/resize:fit:780/1*keS37tRigV2DLMy2itYZGQ.png' data-full='https://miro.medium.com/v2/resize:fit:780/1*keS37tRigV2DLMy2itYZGQ.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48387251' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48387251' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(329) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nouvelles.umontreal.ca/en/article/2026/06/02/how-embryos-shape-their-limbs-a-key-discovery-of-genetic-brakes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Embryos shape their limbs: a key discovery of &quot;genetic brakes&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>胚胎成形肢体：遗传“刹车”机制的关键发现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 10:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48387251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48387251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48387251'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers uncover how embryos shape limb development through genetic brakes that prevent overgrowth or mispatterning. The finding helps explain robust development and could inform regenerative medicine or congenital limb disorders. It highlights the programmable brakes as essential to morphogenesis.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究揭示胚胎在肢体发育中通过“遗传刹车”来控制形态，避免过度生长或模式错位。此发现有助于理解发育的鲁棒性，并可能为再生医学和先天性肢体疾病的研究带来新线索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nouvelles.umontreal.ca/public/nouvelles/_processed_/d/b/csm_Creative_image_of_embryonic_stem_cells_3d7a8e7e9c.jpg' data-full='https://nouvelles.umontreal.ca/public/nouvelles/_processed_/d/b/csm_Creative_image_of_embryonic_stem_cells_3d7a8e7e9c.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48383760' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(330) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medium.com/@carlmkadie/nine-ways-to-do-inheritance-in-rust-a-language-without-inheritance-14825bf1e215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nine Ways to Do Inheritance in Rust, a Language Without Inheritance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust：在没有继承的语言中实现继承的九种方式</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 06:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48383760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48383760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48383760'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Rust-centric guide showing nine patterns to achieve inheritance-like behavior without language-level inheritance, using traits, generics, and composition. It helps Rust developers write flexible, reusable code without classical inheritance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以 Rust 为核心的指南，展示在没有语言内置继承的情况下，通过 trait、泛型与组合实现继承样式的九种模式，帮助开发者写出更灵活、可复用的代码。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://miro.medium.com/v2/resize:fit:1200/1*Gakm8mByVK_pKpz0w027RQ.png' data-full='https://miro.medium.com/v2/resize:fit:1200/1*Gakm8mByVK_pKpz0w027RQ.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(331) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://matse.illinois.edu/news/85775' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A new way to build chips: Sequentially stacking silicon to extend Moore&#x27;s Law</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 30, 2026 / 15:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48341285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48341285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new approach to chip fabrication proposes sequentially stacking silicon to extend Moore&#x27;s Law, enabling higher density and performance via 3D integration. The article outlines potential manufacturing paths, thermal challenges, and roadmap implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出通过逐层堆叠硅晶片来实现更高密度和性能的新芯片制造方法，旨在突破传统平面尺度的极限。文中概述了潜在的制造路线、热管理与良品率等主要技术挑战，以及对产业路线图的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(334) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://surrealdb.com/blog/surrealdb-3-x-by-the-numbers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Benchmarking SurrealDB 3.x vs. Postgres, Mongo, Neo4j and Redis (With Fsync)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SurrealDB 3.x 基准测试：与 Postgres、Mongo、Neo4j 与 Redis（含 fsync）对比</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 29, 2026 / 07:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48323844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48323844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48323844'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SurrealDB 3.x is benchmarked against Postgres, Mongo, Neo4j, and Redis with fsync enabled. The results highlight where SurrealDB shines and how fsync affects durability versus throughput in mixed workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在开启 fsync 的条件下，将 SurrealDB 3.x 与 Postgres、Mongo、Neo4j、Redis 进行对比基准。结果揭示 SurrealDB 在某些工作负载中的优势，以及 fsync 对持久性与吞吐量的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48440952' data-tags='Security,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48440952' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(335) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.troyhunt.com/1000-data-breaches-later-the-disclosure-lag-is-worse-than-ever/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1k Data Breaches Later, the Disclosure Lag Is Worse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>经历千起数据泄露后，披露延迟更严重</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 20:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48440952'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of 1,000 breaches shows the disclosure lag is worse than ever, delaying risk awareness and response. The lengthy gap undermines timely remediation and transparent risk communication. The takeaway: invest in faster detection, real-time telemetry, and stronger breach notification practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对1000起数据泄露的分析显示披露延迟比以往更长，延缓风险识别与应对。长时间的间隔削弱了及时修复和透明的风险沟通。核心要点是加强快速检测、实时遥测，以及更强的泄露通知机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/fb/33/fb3391dc-723d-4e74-b95a-d641b5feb38e/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/photo_2026-05-31_20-34-34.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/fb/33/fb3391dc-723d-4e74-b95a-d641b5feb38e/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/photo_2026-05-31_20-34-34.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48369416' data-tags='Science,Health,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48432722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48432722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kyushu presents a self-hostable WebAssembly sandbox designed for JavaScript workers, enabling isolated execution without relying on external runtimes. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kyushu 提供一个面向 JavaScript 工作者的自托管 WASM 沙箱，允许在不依赖外部运行时的情况下进行隔离执行，提升安全性与可移植性，适用于边缘负载和插件生态。反映出对可重复使用、保护隐私的运行时的需求增长。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(341) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/shadow-walker-biped-humanoid-robot' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DIY Bipedal Robot Used Pneumatic &quot;Air-Muscles&quot; Instead of Motors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DIY Bipedal Robot Used Pneumatic &quot;Air-Muscles&quot; Instead of Motors</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 06:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48345409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48345409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A DIY bipedal humanoid robot uses pneumatic air muscles instead of electric motors, trading precise torque for compliant, responsive motion. The project outlines hardware design and control challenges, offering a look at alternative actuation approaches in robotics. A fascinating take on soft robotics in practice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这款自制双足机器人采用气动“空气肌肉”替代电动机驱动，强调柔顺性和响应性。文中介绍了机械设计与控制难点，展示了机器人驱动的另外一种可行路径。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 13:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48438212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48438212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48438212'>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48372547' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48372547' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(347) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://research.perplexity.ai/articles/rethinking-search-as-code-generation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rethinking search as code generation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把搜索看作代码生成</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 09:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48372547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48372547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48372547'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues for reframing search queries as code-generation tasks, enabling LLMs to emit structured queries, scripts, or APIs to fetch results. It discusses potential gains in accuracy and explainability, plus challenges around latency, privacy, and toolchain integration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张把搜索请求转化为代码生成任务，让大模型输出结构化查询、脚本或接口来获取结果，同时讨论在可解释性、延迟、隐私与工具链整合等方面的潜在挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48345822' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(348) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hannesweissteiner.com/pdfs/frost.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FROST: Fingerprinting Remotely using OPFS-based SSD Timing [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FROST：基于 OPFS 的 SSD 时序可进行远程指纹识别</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 31, 2026 / 07:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48345822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48345822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48345822'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FROST presents a method to fingerprint devices remotely by analyzing SSD timing via the File System Access API (OPFS), revealing a novel web-side channel risk. Defenses include reducing timing leakage and restricting access to low-level IO.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FROST 提出通过 OPFS 的 SSD 时序实现对设备的远程指纹识别，揭示一种新的网页侧信道风险。防御包括降低时序泄露和限制对底层 IO 的访问。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48440549' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48440549' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(351) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://algorithmichiring.github.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Algorithmic Monocultures in Hiring</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>招聘中的算法单一化问题</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 18:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440549' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440549' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48440549'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project argues that automated hiring pipelines can create algorithmic monocultures by over-filtering for narrow signals. It warns of bias amplification and reduced diversity, and advocates for transparent features, human-in-the-loop checks, and diverse training data. The takeaway is to design hiring tools that broaden rather than narrow candidate pools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目指出自动化招聘流程可能通过过度依赖狭窄信号而形成算法性单一化。它警示偏见放大和多样性下降，并主张采用透明特征、人工干预和多样化训练数据。核心要点是让招聘工具拓宽而非缩窄候选人池。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://algorithmichiring.github.io/figures/fig1_pipeline.png' data-full='https://algorithmichiring.github.io/figures/fig1_pipeline.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 20:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48365509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48365509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48365509'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GoPro issued a warning about its going-concern status, citing financial strain and strategic challenges; the company may need to pivot away from core products or invest in new bets like AI memory or services. The note underscores the volatility in hardware startups and the risk of extinction if cash burn persists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GoPro 发布进入持续经营存疑的警告，称财务压力与战略挑战可能威胁其生存。若现金消耗居高不下，公司或需调整路线、寻求新业务方向，此事凸显硬件初创公司在不确定市场中的风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.thenextweb.com/2026/06/gopro-going-concern-ai-memory-crisis-default.avif' data-full='https://media.thenextweb.com/2026/06/gopro-going-concern-ai-memory-crisis-default.avif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(357) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/remysucre/prela' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Prela – Purely Algebraic Relation Combinators</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Prela：纯代数关系组合子</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 06:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48356563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48356563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48356563'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Prela provides a purely algebraic framework for relation combinators, enabling declarative construction and manipulation of relations without procedural code. The approach could influence databases, program analysis, and symbolic computation by offering a solid mathematical foundation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Prela 提供一个纯代数框架，用于关系的组合子，强调以声明式方式构造与操作关系，而非依赖过程性代码。这一思路有望为数据库、程序分析与符号计算提供坚实的数学基础。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48398689' data-tags='Programming,Data,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48398689' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(358) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fremaconsulting.ch/blog/faiss' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Inside FAISS: Billion-Scale Similarity Search</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FAISS内幕：亿级相似性搜索</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 06:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48398689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48398689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48398689'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look inside FAISS, the billion-scale similarity search library, covering indexing strategies, GPU acceleration, and practical considerations for building scalable vector search systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>揭示 FAISS 在亿级规模下实现高效相似性搜索的原理，包括索引策略、GPU 加速等要点，并讨论构建可扩展向量检索系统的实际要点。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(370) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://signoregalilei.com/2026/05/31/youll-never-guess-who-made-the-first-wireless-telephone/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You&#x27;ll never guess who made the first wireless telephone</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A historical look at the surprising inventor behind the first wireless telephone, challenging popular myths with new archival detail. The piece traces early radio experiments and the messy, collaborative nature of telecom breakthroughs that predate wired phones. It reframes how we think about the origins of wireless communication.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章揭示了第一台无线电话的真实发明者，颠覆常见误解并给出历史背景。文中回顾早期无线电实验及通信技术的演进过程，强调发明往往是多人协作的结果，早于有线电话的实现。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48422451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48422451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48422451'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Azure Linux Desktop weaves together Windows-centric tooling (WSLc, WinUI) with Linux desktop capabilities under Azure Linux 4.0 to deliver a cloud-based, GUI-driven Linux experience. The mashup signals how cloud providers are blurring lines between OS ecosystems and easing desktop workflows in the cloud.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Azure Linux 桌面将 Windows 生态工具（WSLc、WinUI）与 Linux 桌面能力在 Azure Linux 4.0 下融合，提供云端的图形界面化 Linux 体验。这种混合趋势显示云服务商正在模糊操作系统生态的边界，并简化云端桌面工作流。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(377) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://louwrentius.com/dns-is-for-people-not-for-it-infrastructure.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DNS is for people, not for IT infrastructure</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 17:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48391957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48391957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48391957'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that DNS should prioritize human usability and resilience, not only as an IT backbone. The piece advocates approachable naming schemes and user-centric DNS design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张 DNS 的设计应以人类使用体验和鲁棒性为优先，而不仅仅作为 IT 基础设施。文中倡导更友好的命名方式与以用户为中心的 DNS 设计。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48368338' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(378) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/tarpediem/zenvision-linux' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reverse-Engineered Userspace Driver for Asus ZenVision Lid OLED on Linux&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Linux 上对 Asus ZenVision Lid OLED 的 Linux 用户态驱动进行逆向工程</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 03:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48368338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48368338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48368338'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers released a reverse-engineered Linux userspace driver enabling the ZenVision lid OLED to work under Linux. The project underscores the value of open-source approaches for niche hardware and the friction created by closed vendor stacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者公布了用于 Asus ZenVision Lid OLED 的 Linux 用户态驱动的逆向工程成果，使该显示在 Linux 上可用。项目凸显了开源社区在小众硬件上的价值，同时也暴露了厂商封闭生态的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fbd0a8f4d70d27ea640fa8e9c0eade5162cd6b235be9a9fc4fc821c557bd2a96/tarpediem/zenvision-linux' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fbd0a8f4d70d27ea640fa8e9c0eade5162cd6b235be9a9fc4fc821c557bd2a96/tarpediem/zenvision-linux' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48373954' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48373954' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(379) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://irfanali.org/blog/zcom' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>No Let, No Rec, No Problem: A Gentler Introduction to the Y and Z Combinators</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不需要 let、也不用递归：更友好的 Y 与 Z 组合子入门</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 11:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48373954' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48373954' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48373954'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Y and Z fixed-point combinators let you express recursion without named functions. A gentler introduction walks through intuition and practical examples, showing how to implement recursive behavior in languages without native recursion or let-bindings. The takeaway is a clearer mental model of fixed-point theory and how to simulate self-reference in simple terms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Y 与 Z 固定点组合子让你在没有显式递归的情况下实现自引用。本文用更易懂的讲解和示例，演示在没有 let 绑定或显式递归的语言中如何实现递归行为，帮助读者建立对固定点原理的直观理解。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(380) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://trifectatech.org/blog/announcing-zstandard-in-rust/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Announcing Zstandard in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 中的 Zstandard 实现发布</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 06:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48356381' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48356381' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48356381'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Rust implementation of the Zstandard compression algorithm is announced, offering high performance with Rust&#x27;s safety guarantees. The project could provide a drop-in replacement for C libraries with safer abstractions and stronger ecosystem integration. It may attract adoption in Rust projects needing fast compression.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Rust 中实现 Zstandard 压缩算法的公告，结合高性能与 Rust 的内存安全特性。该实现有望为需要快速压缩的 Rust 项目提供更安全、易于集成的替代方案。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(383) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/tejaswigowda/ffmpeg-webCLI' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: FFmpeg WebCLI – Full FFmpeg in Browser, Offline PWA, No Uploads(WASM)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：FFmpeg WebCLI — 浏览器中完整 FFmpeg、离线 PWA、无需上传（WASM）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 13:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48404224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48404224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48404224'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FFmpeg WebCLI brings the full FFmpeg toolset to the browser as a WASM-based offline PWA with no uploads required. This enables client-side video processing without sending data to the cloud, though it comes with performance tradeoffs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FFmpeg WebCLI 将完整版 FFmpeg 带入浏览器，以 WASM 构建的离线 PWA 形式运行且无需上传视频。方便在本地完成视频转码，保护隐私，但对设备性能有一定制约。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48397199' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48397199' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(387) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://utcc.utoronto.ca/~cks/space/blog/unix/SuAsLoginReplacement' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When su replaced login for becoming another Unix login</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>su 替代登录成为另一个 Unix 用户的探讨</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 04:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48397199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48397199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48397199'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post explains how the su command can substitute the current session for another user, effectively bypassing a full login, and contrasts this with authentic login behavior. It discusses security implications, auditing, and why sudo is often preferred for traceable privilege elevation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章解释了如何通过 su 命令将当前会话替换为另一个用户的身份，从而在某种程度上绕过完整登录，并对比了与真正登录的行为差异。讨论了安全性、审计以及在日常运维中为何更应偏好 sudo 以实现可追踪的特权提升。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48322134' data-tags='Career,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48322134' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(390) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/chip-design-academic-vs-industry' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Finding success in industry as a chip designer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在芯片设计行业取得成功</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 29, 2026 / 05:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48322134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48322134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48322134'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece contrasts academic paths vs industry for chip designers, emphasizing practical engineering experience, fab collaboration, and end-to-end system thinking. It offers career strategies for researchers aiming to transition into impactful hardware roles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章对比了学术路径与业界在芯片设计中的发展，强调实践工程经验、与晶圆厂的协作以及端到端的系统思维的重要性。为希望进入有影响力的硬件岗位的研究人员提供了职业策略。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tutorial-style reconstruction of Warren&#x27;s Abstract Machine (WAM), illustrating how Prolog-style execution can be modeled and implemented. It bridges theory and practical interpreter design for language enthusiasts.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(393) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.patrickstevens.co.uk/posts/2026-06-04-announcing-woofware-pawprint/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WoofWare.PawPrint, a Deterministic .NET Runtime</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WoofWare.PawPrint：一个确定性的 .NET 运行时</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 00:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48395370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48395370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48395370'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WoofWare.PawPrint is a deterministic .NET runtime aimed at reproducible results and safer concurrent execution. It appeals to developers needing predictable builds for testing, formal verification, or embedded contexts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WoofWare.PawPrint 是一个确定性的 .NET 运行时，旨在实现可重复的执行结果，降低时序与并发带来的不确定性。对需要可预测构建、形式化验证或嵌入式场景的开发者具有吸引力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.patrickstevens.co.uk/images/AboutMe/profile.png' data-full='https://www.patrickstevens.co.uk/images/AboutMe/profile.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>因拒绝表亲婚嫁而被杀：一名15岁亲属的遭遇</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 13:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48362364' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48362364' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48362364'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 15-year-old relative was killed for refusing forced marriage to a cousin, highlighting ongoing gender-based violence and failures in child protection. The tragedy underscores human-rights concerns and the need for stronger safeguards for vulnerable girls in affected regions. The case has drawn international attention to local and cultural dynamics surrounding forced marriage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名15岁亲属因拒绝被迫与表亲结婚而遇害，突显持续的性别暴力与未成年人保护体系的缺失。这一悲剧凸显人权问题和对受影响地区弱势女孩更强保护的需求，引发国际社会对当地文化与强迫婚姻问题的关注。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(402) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Efficient and Training-Free Single-Image Diffusion Models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>高效且无需训练的单图扩散模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 02:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48433305' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48433305' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48433305'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A method for obtaining efficient, training-free diffusion outputs from a single image, pushing diffusion models toward zero-shot or few-shot adaptation. This reduces compute and data requirements while maintaining quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出一种通过单图实现高效、无需额外训练的扩散模型输出的方法，推进零-shot/少样本自适应，降低计算与数据需求，同时保持输出质量。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该研究尝试把可解释的离散模型与强大连续生成模型结合起来，提出统一决策树与扩散模型的方法，或提升可解释性并为混合架构提供新思路。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(406) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/zaydmulani09/mnemo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Mnemo – local-first AI memory layer for any LLM (Rust, SQLite,petgraph)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mnemo：本地优先的 AI 记忆层，面向任意大型语言模型（Rust、SQLite、petgraph）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 13:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48389586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48389586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48389586'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mnemo provides a local-first memory layer for LLMs, enabling on-device or local-then-sync memory storage using Rust, SQLite, and petgraph. This approach improves privacy and latency and can reduce reliance on external vector databases. It could become a building block for offline-capable AI apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mnemo 为 LLM 提供本地优先的记忆层，利用 Rust、SQLite 和 petgraph 实现本地存储并可与云端同步。提高隐私与低延迟，降低对外部向量数据库的依赖，有望成为离线 AI 应用的基础组件。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(409) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.natesilver.net/p/why-isnt-the-us-better-at-soccer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why isn&#x27;t the U.S. better at soccer?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国为何在足球方面表现不如人意？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 12:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48437848'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Silver analyzes structural factors behind the US soccer performance, including talent development pipelines, league competitiveness, and grassroots investment, arguing that systemic reforms are needed for long-term improvement. The piece emphasizes data-driven, policy-backed approaches to grow the sport.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尼特·西尔弗分析了美国足球表现不佳的结构性原因，如青训体系、联赛竞争力和草根投入等，并提出需要通过长期、系统性的改革来提升水平，强调以数据驱动和政策支持来推进运动发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!-M0f!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1a50f2e4-1f55-4570-a1e0-add3a882942b_1200x772.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!-M0f!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1a50f2e4-1f55-4570-a1e0-add3a882942b_1200x772.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48435371' data-tags='AI,Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48435371' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(410) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://leoveanu.com/2026-06-06-qwen3.7max/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The OnlyFans Economy of American AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国 AI 的 OnlyFans 经济</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 07:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48435371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48435371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48435371'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look at how the AI industry in the US is sustained by a creator-economy style labor model, where data labeling, model training, and content contributions are commodified. The piece argues that this &#x27;OnlyFans&#x27; style economy shapes who benefits, pressures workers, and influences AI pricing and governance; implications for regulation and equity in AI development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>聚焦美国 AI 行业如何通过创作者经济式劳动模式维持运作：数据标注、模型训练与内容贡献被商品化。文章认为这种“OnlyFans 风格”的经济模式决定了谁受益、对工人施加压力，并影响 AI 定价与治理，对监管和公平性具有重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://leoveanu.com/assets/2026-06-06-qwen3.7max/cover.png' data-full='https://leoveanu.com/assets/2026-06-06-qwen3.7max/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48437450' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48437450' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(414) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://knowablemagazine.org/content/article/living-world/2026/low-protein-diet-animals-live-longer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The curious case of low-protein diets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>低蛋白饮食的奥秘：动物为何更长寿</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 11:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48437450'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The curious case of low-protein diets discusses findings that reduced protein intake can extend lifespan in some animals, likely via nutrient-sensing pathways. It weighs trade-offs in reproduction and health and considers what this could mean for aging research in humans. The piece situates the results in a broader ecological and evolutionary context.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Knowable Magazine 深度报道显示，在某些动物模型中，降低蛋白质摄入可能延长寿命，常与代谢通路如 mTOR、胰岛素/生长因子有关。文章探讨了繁殖、健康与生态环境的权衡，以及这些发现对人类衰老研究的潜在启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://knowablemagazine.org/docserver/fulltext/protein-restriction-1600x600.jpg' data-full='https://knowablemagazine.org/docserver/fulltext/protein-restriction-1600x600.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(415) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/s2-streamstore/replaya' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: RePlaya – self-hosted browser session replay with live tailing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: RePlaya – 自托管的浏览器会话回放与实时尾随</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 10:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48373482' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48373482' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48373482'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RePlaya is a self-hosted browser session replay tool with live tailing, enabling private debugging of user sessions without third-party services. It appeals to teams prioritizing data sovereignty and reproducibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RePlaya 是一个自托管的浏览器会话回放工具，具备实时尾随功能，支持在不依赖第三方服务的前提下进行私密调试，适合重视数据主权与可重复性的团队。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>sqlite: A CGO-free port of SQLite/SQLite3 describes a CGO-free embedding of SQLite, reducing binary dependencies and making it easier to build in environments without CGo. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目提供了一个无 CGo 的 SQLite/SQLite3 端口，便于在不依赖 cgo 的环境中嵌入数据库。它降低了二进制依赖与跨编译难度，但可能在性能或兼容性上有权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(422) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kotaku.com/microsoft-ai-scout-addictive-satya-nadella-404-media-copilot-2000702924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Leak Reveals Microsoft Wants Its AI to Be &#x27;Addictive&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>泄露称微软欲使其 AI 具备‘上瘾性’</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 05, 2026 / 08:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48413924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48413924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48413924'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A leaked report suggests Microsoft aims to make its AI products highly addictive, raising concerns about user wellbeing, monetization, and responsibility. The document highlights tensions between engagement-driven design and ethical AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>泄露文件称微软希望其 AI 产品具备高度吸引力，引发对用户福祉、商业化与负责任 AI 的担忧；文本揭示了以增长黏性为导向的设计与伦理之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kotaku.com/app/uploads/2026/06/scout2-1200x675.jpeg' data-full='https://kotaku.com/app/uploads/2026/06/scout2-1200x675.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48384810' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48384810' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(423) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/06/03/tesla-retroactively-modified-fsd-contracts-supervised/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla retroactively added &#x27;supervised&#x27; to FSD contracts owners signed years ago</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉回溯性地在 FSD 合同中加入‘受监督’</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 07:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48384810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48384810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48384810'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tesla retroactively added &#x27;supervised&#x27; to FSD contracts, altering the user agreement to require supervision for autonomy. The move raises questions about consumer rights, software liability, and the evolving definition of driver assistance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特斯拉回溯性地在 FSD 合同中加入“受监督”条款，要求在自动驾驶情况下需要监督。此举引发关于消费者权利、软件责任和驾驶辅助定义演变的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2026/06/Tesla-FSD-supervised-document-retroactively.png?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2026/06/Tesla-FSD-supervised-document-retroactively.png?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48346766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48346766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48346766'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video demonstrates loading Sega games from a vinyl record data encoding, blending analog media with digital games. The stunt illustrates creative data encoding and the longevity of physical media for experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>视频展示了通过黑胶唱片中的数据编码加载世嘉游戏的实验，将模拟介质与数字游戏数据进行有趣的融合。</p>
