<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48375673' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48375673' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(425) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://julienreszka.com/blog/rss-is-back-ai-agents-are-reading-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Now AI agents need what RSS does</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 代理现在需要像 RSS 那样的输入流</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 13:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48375673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48375673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48375673'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI agents rely on feed-like data streams to stay current; RSS-like inputs offer structured, low-latency content with metadata that agents can parse and reason over. The piece argues that traditional web feeds may become essential plumbing for autonomous systems and copilots.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张 AI 代理需要像 RSS 那样的输入流来获取结构化、可预测的内容，便于解析和推理。此类 feed 式的数据源可能成为自动化系统和协作助手的关键底层。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://julienreszka.com/blog/rss-is-back-ai-agents-are-reading-it/og.png' data-full='https://julienreszka.com/blog/rss-is-back-ai-agents-are-reading-it/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(426) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://magenta.withgoogle.com/magenta-realtime-2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Magenta RealTime 2: Open and Local Live Music Models</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 21:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48407815' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48407815' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48407815'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Magenta RealTime 2 introduces open and local live music models that run on-device, addressing latency and privacy concerns while enabling AI-assisted performance. The approach supports on-device inference and creative experimentation without cloud dependencies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Magenta RealTime 2 引入开源的本地实时音乐模型，可在设备上运行，解决了延迟和隐私问题，同时支持 AI 辅助演出。此方法实现了本地推断，降低对云端依赖，促进创意实验。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 30, 2026 / 01:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48333823' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48333823' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48333823'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SLAX offers a script-style syntax for XML transformations, offering a friendlier alternative to traditional XSLT. It aims to speed up XML processing and improve readability for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SLAX 提供了一种脚本化的 XML 转换语法，作为传统 XSLT 的替代方案。设计目标是加速 XML 处理并提升开发者的可读性。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48385829'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ideogram 4.0 releases open-weight 9.3B text-to-image model, advancing accessible, community-driven AI art generation. The open weights enable researchers and hobbyists to run and fine-tune the model locally, fostering experimentation and transparency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ideogram 4.0 发布开源权重的 9.3B 文图模型，推动了更易获取的 AI 艺术生成。开源权重使研究者和爱好者能够本地运行与微调模型，促进实验与透明度。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(430) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mojodojo.io/blog/google-just-made-you-a-search-quality-rater-you-won-t-get-paid' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google just made you a search quality rater. You won&#x27;t get paid</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌让你成为搜索质量评分员：你不会得到酬劳</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 07, 2026 / 05:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48434001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48434001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48434001'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google just made you a search quality rater. You won&#x27;t get paid explains how Google recruits unpaid raters to help refine search quality signals. It raises questions about compensation, data labeling norms, and the living boundary between crowdsourcing and paid work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌现在招募无薪的搜索质量评估员，让人类判断来微调排名信号。该计划带来规模化反馈的潜力，同时引发对报酬、公平性、数据标注流程及众包任务的关注。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48430282' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48430282' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(431) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gwern.net/llm-catapult' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Human-Like Neural Nets by Catapulting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过跃升实现的类人神经网络</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 16:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48430282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48430282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48430282'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discusses how to push toward more human-like neural nets via targeted leaps in capability, through scaling, training dynamics, and architectural tweaks. It surveys promising approaches and early results, arguing that deliberate breakthroughs can yield more robust, human-like reasoning without a full model rewrite.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨通过“跃升”式的办法让神经网络更具类人特征，可能涉及扩展规模、训练策略和结构调整等。文章梳理了若干方法及初步结果，认为在不全面重做模型的前提下进行有意的跃升也能带来更强的推理和鲁棒性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gwern.net/doc/reinforcement-learning/model-free/2008-12-07-rogerjohansson-geneticprogrammingevolutionofmonalisa-figure1-2x2subset-scalingoptimization3oomsshowsimprovementinrenderingmonalisawith50semitransparentpolygons.png' data-full='https://gwern.net/doc/reinforcement-learning/model-free/2008-12-07-rogerjohansson-geneticprogrammingevolutionofmonalisa-figure1-2x2subset-scalingoptimization3oomsshowsimprovementinrenderingmonalisawith50semitransparentpolygons.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48355607' data-tags='Hardware,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48355607' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(432) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://derwiki.medium.com/now-is-the-best-time-to-be-a-duct-tape-engineer-eefc1d141c23' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Now Is the Best Time to Be a Duct Tape Engineer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现在是成为“胶带工程师”的最佳时机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 04:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48355607' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48355607' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48355607'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Now Is the Best Time to Be a Duct Tape Engineer argues that today&#x27;s toolkit, from cheap parts to open hardware and rapid prototyping, enables engineers to ship ideas quickly with pragmatic tradeoffs. It champions resourcefulness, cross-disciplinary tinkering, and the value of low-cost, iterative solutions that accelerate innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为当下的工程环境为快速原型开发提供了充足工具：廉价零件、开源工具和快速迭代让工程师能够用有限资源快速落地方案。强调务实、跨学科协作，以及低成本、可重复性的迭代解决方案在推动创新中的价值。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 17:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48406530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48406530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48406530'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Yon is a topos-inspired programming language featuring a content-addressed lattice heap, aiming for formal semantics and persistent data structures. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Yon 是一个受 topos 启发的编程语言，使用内容寻址的格堆，强调形式化语义和持久数据结构。其设计可能在分布式系统和对代码进行严格推理方面有潜在应用，对语言理论和系统研究者具有吸引力。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 02, 2026 / 00:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48367223' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48367223' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>California&#x27;s public university system embraced AI aggressively, funding labs and integrating AI tools into curricula. The case study signals how large-scale AI adoption can collide with state politics and institutional culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州公立大学系统大力推动 AI，投资实验室并将 AI 工具融入课程。但治理、资金分配和就业影响等方面的分歧让校园内部出现紧张与冲突。这起案例凸显了大规模 AI 采用可能与州层面政治和制度文化产生冲突。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Computex 2026 scenes hint at a shift toward agentic PCs—hardware capable of autonomous AI-assisted tasks, propping up new software-hardware ecosystems. The story weighs hype against practical constraints like privacy, security, and developer tooling, exploring whether consumer PCs will become proactive assistants.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(453) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://groups.csail.mit.edu/medg/people/psz/Licklider.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Man-Computer Symbiosis J. C. R. Licklider (1960)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人–机共生：J. C. R. 里克莱德（1960）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 04, 2026 / 16:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48405890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48405890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48405890'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>J. C. R. Licklider&#x27;s 1960 essay argued for a partnership between humans and computers, envisioning cooperative problem-solving and cumulative intelligence through networks. The concept underpins modern HCI and AI systems, shaping how we design interactive tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1960 年，洛克利德提出了人机共生的设想，强调人和计算机携手协作、通过网络实现协同智能。该观点奠定了现代人机交互与人工智能系统的设计思路，影响至今。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 06, 2026 / 05:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48424252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48424252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta is extending the timeline for developer access to its new AI model, signaling cautious rollout, risk management, and a strategic cadence in the competitive AI landscape. The delay underscores how access schemes shape ecosystems around large-language-model platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 延长新 AI 模型向开发者开放的时间表，显示出更谨慎的推出节奏、风险管理以及在竞争性 AI 生态中的策略性步伐。此举凸显了访问机制如何影响大模型生态。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(461) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/ai-video-games-llms-togelius' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why are large language models so terrible at video games?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么大型语言模型在电子游戏中表现如此糟糕？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 02:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48354405' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48354405' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48354405'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LLMs struggle with action-heavy tasks due to lack of embodied feedback and motor control; the article examines why this mismatch occurs and what improvements might help LLMs play games better.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大型语言模型在需要实时行动的电子游戏中表现不佳，原因在于缺乏具身经验和感知反馈。文章分析了这一错配的根源，以及可能让 LLMs 更擅长游戏的改进方向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=65413499&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=50%2C514%2C293%2C907' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=65413499&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=50%2C514%2C293%2C907' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Writing Portable ARM64 Assembly (2023) explores how to craft ARM64 assembly that stays portable across Apple, Android, and Linux ecosystems. It covers ABI conventions, calling conventions, and pragmatic patterns to maintain compatibility on multiple platforms. A practical guide for low-level cross-platform code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Writing Portable ARM64 Assembly (2023)》探讨如何编写在 Apple、Android、Linux 等平台间可移植的 ARM64 汇编。内容涵盖 ABI 与调用约定，以及在不同体系结构上保持兼容性的实用模式。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48418721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48418721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48418721'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Economist piece argues Texas has become a new anchor for corporate America, highlighting business-friendly policy, energy, and talent trends that draw companies from coastal regions. It analyzes macro implications for investment and supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该篇经济学人文章认为德州已成为美国企业的新核心，强调友好的政策环境、能源优势与人才流动等因素吸引企业从沿海地区迁入，并分析对投资与供应链的宏观影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48361054' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(464) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gettextile.app' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Textile – A desktop app for weaving together bits of text</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Textile——用于拼接文本片段的桌面应用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 01, 2026 / 11:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48361054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48361054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48361054'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Textile is a desktop app designed to weave together disparate text snippets into a coherent whole, aiding brainstorming and drafting workflows. It emphasizes a lightweight, local-first approach for organizing fragments of ideas. The app targets writers and developers seeking a tactile, offline text-crafting workflow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Textile 是一款桌面应用，旨在把零散的文本片段拼接成一个连贯的整体，帮助头脑风暴与起草工作流。它强调轻量化、就地存储的文本组织方法，面向需要直观离线文本创作流程的作者与开发者。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48330696' data-tags='Science,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48330696' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(465) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://convergentthinking.sh/posts/bias-compounds-variance-washes-out/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bias Compounds, Variance Washes Out</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>偏差叠加，方差被冲淡</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 29, 2026 / 16:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48330696' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48330696' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48330696'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In certain data regimes, bias compounds as data grows while variance effects fade, challenging conventional bias-variance intuition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在某些数据情形下，随着数据量增加，偏差会叠加而方差效应却逐渐减弱，这对传统的偏差-方差观念构成挑战。文中讨论对评估和数据收集策略的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://convergentthinking.sh/posts/bias-compounds-variance-washes-out/accumulation.png' data-full='https://convergentthinking.sh/posts/bias-compounds-variance-washes-out/accumulation.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48385866' data-tags='Business,Finance,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48385866' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(466) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.irishtimes.com/business/2026/06/03/heavily-in-debt-loss-making-with-eyes-on-sending-people-to-mars-why-would-anyone-invest-in-spacex/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SpaceX&#x27;s IPO is a disaster waiting to happen for your pension fund</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊朗战争令美国石油储备降至2004年以来的最低水平</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 14:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48390053' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48390053' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48390053'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Geopolitical tension with Iran is draining U.S. oil stocks to the lowest level since 2004, tightening supply and pushing prices higher. The shift affects energy costs, inflation, and the cost structure of tech hardware, data centers, and other energy-intensive operations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>与伊朗的地缘政治紧张局势使美国的石油库存降至2004年以来的最低水平，供应趋紧、价格上涨。此变化影响能源成本、通胀，以及对科技硬件、数据中心等高耗能行业的成本结构。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(475) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/loadingalias/rscrypto' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Rscrypto, pure-Rust crypto with industry leading public benches</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Rscrypto，纯 Rust 的密码学库，具行业领先的公开基准</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 09:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48386343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48386343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48386343'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rscrypto is a pure-Rust cryptography library with public benchmarks, aiming for transparent, verifiable performance. The project appeals to security-conscious developers who want reliable crypto in Rust without vendor-specific optimizations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rscrypto 是一个纯 Rust 的密码学库，提供公开的基准以实现透明可核验的性能。该项目面向重视安全性的开发者，追求在 Rust 中获得可靠且可验证的加密功能。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(476) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newsletter.maartengrootendorst.com/p/a-visual-guide-to-gemma-4-12b' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Visual Guide to Gemma 4 12B</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemma 4 12B 的可视化指南</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Jun 03, 2026 / 09:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48386221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48386221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48386221'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 12B is a 12B-parameter model. A visual guide explains its architecture, training data, capabilities, and limitations using diagrams and charts. It helps readers understand how the model behaves and highlights safety and deployment considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 12B 是一个 12B 参数的模型，可视化指南通过图示解释其架构、训练数据、能力与局限性，便于读者理解模型的工作机制。内容有助于评估其安全性与落地应用时的风险点。</p>
